1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→PLAN_VERSION = 2
    10→
    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"}
    12→
    13→
    14→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    15→    finding_id: Required[str]
    16→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    17→    reason: str | None
    18→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    19→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    20→    created_at: str
    21→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    22→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    23→
    24→
    25→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    26→    finding_id: Required[str]
    27→    description: str | None
    28→    note: str | None
    29→    cluster: str | None
    30→    created_at: str
    31→    updated_at: str
    32→
    33→
    34→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    35→    name: Required[str]
    36→    description: str | None
    37→    finding_ids: list[str]
    38→    created_at: str
    39→    updated_at: str
    40→
    41→
    42→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    43→    original_id: Required[str]
    44→    original_detector: str
    45→    original_file: str
    46→    original_summary: str
    47→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    48→    superseded_at: str
    49→    remapped_to: str | None
    50→    candidates: list[str]
    51→    note: str | None
    52→
    53→
    54→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    55→    version: Required[int]
    56→    created: Required[str]
    57→    updated: Required[str]
    58→    queue_order: list[str]
    59→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    60→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    61→    active_cluster: str | None
    62→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
    63→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    64→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
    65→
    66→
    67→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
    68→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
    69→    now = utc_now()
    70→    return {
    71→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
    72→        "created": now,
    73→        "updated": now,
    74→        "queue_order": [],
    75→        "deferred": [],
    76→        "skipped": {},
    77→        "active_cluster": None,
    78→        "overrides": {},
    79→        "clusters": {},
    80→        "superseded": {},
    81→    }
    82→
    83→
    84→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    85→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
    86→
    87→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict).
    88→    """
    89→    defaults = empty_plan()
    90→    for key, value in defaults.items():
    91→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
    92→
    93→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
    94→        plan["queue_order"] = []
    95→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
    96→        plan["deferred"] = []
    97→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
    98→        plan["skipped"] = {}
    99→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   100→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   101→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   102→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   103→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   104→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   105→
   106→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   107→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   108→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   109→    if deferred:
   110→        now = utc_now()
   111→        for fid in list(deferred):
   112→            if fid not in skipped:
   113→                skipped[fid] = {
   114→                    "finding_id": fid,
   115→                    "kind": "temporary",
   116→                    "reason": None,
   117→                    "note": None,
   118→                    "attestation": None,
   119→                    "created_at": now,
   120→                    "review_after": None,
   121→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   122→                }
   123→        deferred.clear()
   124→
   125→    # Normalize cluster finding_ids
   126→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   127→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   128→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   129→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   130→
   131→
   132→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   133→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   134→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   135→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   136→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   137→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   138→
   139→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   140→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   141→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   142→    if overlap:
   143→        raise ValueError(
   144→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   145→        )
   146→
   147→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   148→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   149→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   150→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   151→            raise ValueError(
   152→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   153→            )
   154→
   155→
   156→__all__ = [
   157→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   158→    "Cluster",
   159→    "ItemOverride",
   160→    "PlanModel",
   161→    "SkipEntry",
   162→    "SupersededEntry",
   163→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   164→    "empty_plan",
   165→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   166→    "validate_plan",
   167→]
   168→

1→"""Plan persistence — load/save with atomic writes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import shutil
     8→import sys
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    PLAN_VERSION,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    empty_plan,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→    validate_plan,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import STATE_DIR, json_default
    20→
    21→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22→
    23→PLAN_FILE = STATE_DIR / "plan.json"
    24→
    25→
    26→def load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel:
    27→    """Load plan from disk, or return empty plan on missing/corruption."""
    28→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    29→    if not plan_path.exists():
    30→        return empty_plan()
    31→
    32→    try:
    33→        data = json.loads(plan_path.read_text())
    34→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as ex:
    35→        print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    36→        return empty_plan()
    37→
    38→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    39→        print("  Warning: Plan file root must be a JSON object. Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    40→        return empty_plan()
    41→
    42→    version = data.get("version", 1)
    43→    if version > PLAN_VERSION:
    44→        print(
    45→            f"  Warning: Plan file version {version} is newer than supported "
    46→            f"({PLAN_VERSION}). Some features may not work correctly.",
    47→            file=sys.stderr,
    48→        )
    49→
    50→    ensure_plan_defaults(data)
    51→    try:
    52→        validate_plan(data)
    53→    except ValueError as ex:
    54→        print(f"  Warning: Plan invariants invalid ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    55→        return empty_plan()
    56→
    57→    return data  # type: ignore[return-value]
    58→
    59→
    60→def save_plan(plan: PlanModel | dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    61→    """Validate and save plan to disk atomically."""
    62→    from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    63→
    64→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    65→    plan["updated"] = utc_now()
    66→    validate_plan(plan)
    67→
    68→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    69→    plan_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    70→
    71→    content = json.dumps(plan, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
    72→
    73→    if plan_path.exists():
    74→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    75→        try:
    76→            shutil.copy2(str(plan_path), str(backup))
    77→        except OSError as backup_ex:
    78→            logger.debug("Failed to create plan backup: %s", backup_ex)
    79→
    80→    try:
    81→        safe_write_text(plan_path, content)
    82→    except OSError as ex:
    83→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save plan: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
    84→        raise
    85→
    86→
    87→def plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path:
    88→    """Derive plan.json path from a state file path."""
    89→    return state_path.parent / "plan.json"
    90→
    91→
    92→def has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool:
    93→    """Return True if a plan.json exists and has user intent."""
    94→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    95→    if not plan_path.exists():
    96→        return False
    97→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
    98→    return bool(
    99→        plan.get("queue_order")
   100→        or plan.get("deferred")
   101→        or plan.get("overrides")
   102→        or plan.get("clusters")
   103→    )
   104→
   105→
   106→__all__ = [
   107→    "PLAN_FILE",
   108→    "has_living_plan",
   109→    "load_plan",
   110→    "plan_path_for_state",
   111→    "save_plan",
   112→]
   113→

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    10→
    11→
    12→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass
    16→class ReconcileResult:
    17→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    18→
    19→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    20→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    21→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    changes: int = 0
    23→
    24→
    25→def _find_candidates(
    26→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    27→) -> list[str]:
    28→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    29→    candidates: list[str] = []
    30→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    31→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    32→            continue
    33→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    34→            candidates.append(fid)
    35→    return candidates
    36→
    37→
    38→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    39→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    40→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    41→    if finding is None:
    42→        return False
    43→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    44→
    45→
    46→def _supersede_id(
    47→    plan: PlanModel,
    48→    state: StateModel,
    49→    finding_id: str,
    50→    now: str,
    51→) -> bool:
    52→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    53→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    54→    detector = ""
    55→    file = ""
    56→    summary = ""
    57→    if finding:
    58→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    59→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    60→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    61→
    62→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    63→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    64→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    65→
    66→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    67→        "original_id": finding_id,
    68→        "original_detector": detector,
    69→        "original_file": file,
    70→        "original_summary": summary,
    71→        "status": "superseded",
    72→        "superseded_at": now,
    73→        "remapped_to": None,
    74→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    # Preserve any existing override note
    78→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    79→    if override and override.get("note"):
    80→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    81→
    82→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    83→
    84→    # Remove from queue_order, deferred, skipped, cluster finding_ids
    85→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    86→    deferred: list[str] = plan.get("deferred", [])
    87→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    88→    if finding_id in order:
    89→        order.remove(finding_id)
    90→    if finding_id in deferred:
    91→        deferred.remove(finding_id)
    92→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    93→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    94→        ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    95→        if finding_id in ids:
    96→            ids.remove(finding_id)
    97→
    98→    return True
    99→
   100→
   101→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   102→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   103→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   104→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   105→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   106→
   107→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   108→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   109→        try:
   110→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   111→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   112→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   113→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   114→                to_prune.append(fid)
   115→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   116→            to_prune.append(fid)
   117→
   118→    for fid in to_prune:
   119→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   120→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   121→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   122→
   123→    return to_prune
   124→
   125→
   126→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   127→    plan: PlanModel,
   128→    state: StateModel,
   129→) -> ReconcileResult:
   130→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   131→
   132→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   133→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   134→    """
   135→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   136→    result = ReconcileResult()
   137→    now = utc_now()
   138→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   139→
   140→    # Collect all finding IDs referenced by the plan
   141→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   142→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   143→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("deferred", []))
   144→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   145→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   146→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   147→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   148→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   149→
   150→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs
   151→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   152→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   153→
   154→    # Check each referenced ID
   155→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   156→        if not _is_finding_alive(state, fid):
   157→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   158→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   159→                result.changes += 1
   160→
   161→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   162→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   163→    from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import resurface_stale_skips
   164→
   165→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   166→    if resurfaced:
   167→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   168→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   169→
   170→    # Prune old superseded entries
   171→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   172→    result.pruned = pruned
   173→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   174→
   175→    return result
   176→
   177→
   178→__all__ = [
   179→    "ReconcileResult",
   180→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   181→]
   182→

1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import warnings
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     8→    Cluster,
     9→    ItemOverride,
    10→    PlanModel,
    11→    SkipEntry,
    12→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    15→
    16→
    17→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    18→# Helpers
    19→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    20→
    21→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    22→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order, deferred, and skipped."""
    23→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    24→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
    25→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    26→    if finding_id in order:
    27→        order.remove(finding_id)
    28→    if finding_id in deferred:
    29→        deferred.remove(finding_id)
    30→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    31→
    32→
    33→def _resolve_position(
    34→    order: list[str],
    35→    position: str,
    36→    target: str | None = None,
    37→    offset: int | None = None,
    38→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    39→) -> int:
    40→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    41→
    42→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    43→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    44→    """
    45→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    46→
    47→    if position == "top":
    48→        return 0
    49→    if position == "bottom":
    50→        return len(order)
    51→
    52→    if position == "before" and target:
    53→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    54→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    55→                return i
    56→        return 0
    57→
    58→    if position == "after" and target:
    59→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    60→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    61→                return i + 1
    62→        return len(order)
    63→
    64→    if position == "up" and offset is not None:
    65→        if not finding_ids:
    66→            return 0
    67→        # Find current position of first item
    68→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    69→        current_idx = None
    70→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    71→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    72→            if item_id == first_id:
    73→                current_idx = i
    74→                break
    75→        if current_idx is None:
    76→            # Item not yet in order; treat as "insert at position offset from top"
    77→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    78→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    79→
    80→    if position == "down" and offset is not None:
    81→        if not finding_ids:
    82→            return len(order)
    83→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    84→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    85→        current_idx = None
    86→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    87→            if item_id == first_id:
    88→                current_idx = i
    89→                break
    90→        if current_idx is None:
    91→            return len(clean_order)
    92→        return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    93→
    94→    return len(order)
    95→
    96→
    97→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    98→# Move items
    99→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   100→
   101→def move_items(
   102→    plan: PlanModel,
   103→    finding_ids: list[str],
   104→    position: str,
   105→    target: str | None = None,
   106→    offset: int | None = None,
   107→) -> int:
   108→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   109→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   110→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   111→
   112→    # Remove from deferred if present
   113→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   114→    for fid in finding_ids:
   115→        if fid in deferred:
   116→            deferred.remove(fid)
   117→
   118→    # Remove from current position in order
   119→    existing = [fid for fid in order if fid in set(finding_ids)]
   120→    for fid in finding_ids:
   121→        if fid in order:
   122→            order.remove(fid)
   123→
   124→    # Resolve insertion point
   125→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   126→
   127→    # Insert in original order
   128→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   129→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   130→
   131→    return len(finding_ids)
   132→
   133→
   134→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   135→# Skip / unskip
   136→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   137→
   138→def skip_items(
   139→    plan: PlanModel,
   140→    finding_ids: list[str],
   141→    *,
   142→    kind: str = "temporary",
   143→    reason: str | None = None,
   144→    note: str | None = None,
   145→    attestation: str | None = None,
   146→    review_after: int | None = None,
   147→    scan_count: int = 0,
   148→) -> int:
   149→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   150→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   151→    now = utc_now()
   152→    count = 0
   153→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   154→    for fid in finding_ids:
   155→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   156→        skipped[fid] = {
   157→            "finding_id": fid,
   158→            "kind": kind,
   159→            "reason": reason,
   160→            "note": note,
   161→            "attestation": attestation,
   162→            "created_at": now,
   163→            "review_after": review_after,
   164→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   165→        }
   166→        count += 1
   167→    return count
   168→
   169→
   170→def unskip_items(
   171→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   172→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   173→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   174→
   175→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   176→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   177→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   178→    """
   179→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   180→    count = 0
   181→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   182→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   183→    for fid in finding_ids:
   184→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   185→        if entry is not None:
   186→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   187→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   188→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   189→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   190→            count += 1
   191→    return count, need_reopen
   192→
   193→
   194→def resurface_stale_skips(
   195→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   196→) -> list[str]:
   197→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   198→
   199→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   200→    """
   201→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   202→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   203→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   204→    for fid in list(skipped):
   205→        entry = skipped[fid]
   206→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   207→            continue
   208→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   209→        if review_after is None:
   210→            continue
   211→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   212→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   213→            skipped.pop(fid)
   214→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   215→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   216→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   217→    return resurfaced
   218→

   224→def defer_items(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:

   226→
   227→    .. deprecated:: Use :func:`skip_items` instead.

   230→        "defer_items() is deprecated, use skip_items() instead",
   231→        DeprecationWarning,
   232→        stacklevel=2,
   233→    )
   234→    return skip_items(plan, finding_ids, kind="temporary")
   235→
   236→
   237→def undefer_items(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   238→    """Bring finding IDs back from deferred to the end of queue_order.
   239→
   240→    .. deprecated:: Use :func:`unskip_items` instead.

   242→    warnings.warn(
   243→        "undefer_items() is deprecated, use unskip_items() instead",
   244→        DeprecationWarning,
   245→        stacklevel=2,
   246→    )
   247→    count, _ = unskip_items(plan, finding_ids)
   248→    return count

   255→def describe_finding(
   256→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   257→) -> None:

   259→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)

   261→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   262→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   263→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   264→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   265→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now

   270→) -> None:

   272→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)

   274→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   275→    if finding_id not in overrides:

   277→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   278→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now

   284→
   285→def create_cluster(
   286→    plan: PlanModel, name: str, description: str | None = None
   287→) -> Cluster:
   288→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   289→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   290→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   291→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   292→    now = utc_now()
   293→    cluster: Cluster = {
   294→        "name": name,
   295→        "description": description,
   296→        "finding_ids": [],
   297→        "created_at": now,
   298→        "updated_at": now,
   299→    }
   300→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   301→    return cluster
   302→
   303→
   304→def add_to_cluster(
   305→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   306→) -> int:
   307→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   308→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   309→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   310→    if cluster is None:
   311→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   312→
   313→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   314→    count = 0
   315→    now = utc_now()
   316→    for fid in finding_ids:
   317→        if fid not in member_ids:
   318→            member_ids.append(fid)
   319→            count += 1
   320→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   321→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   322→        if fid not in overrides:
   323→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   324→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   325→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   326→
   327→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   328→    return count
   329→
   330→
   331→def remove_from_cluster(
   332→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   333→) -> int:
   334→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   335→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   336→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   337→    if cluster is None:
   338→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   339→
   340→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   341→    now = utc_now()
   342→    count = 0
   343→    for fid in finding_ids:
   344→        if fid in member_ids:
   345→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   346→            count += 1
   347→        # Clear cluster from override
   348→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   349→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   350→            override["cluster"] = None
   351→            override["updated_at"] = now
   352→
   353→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   354→    return count
   355→
   356→
   357→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   358→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   359→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   360→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   361→    if cluster is None:
   362→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   363→
   364→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   365→    now = utc_now()
   366→    for fid in orphaned:
   367→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   368→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   369→            override["cluster"] = None
   370→            override["updated_at"] = now
   371→
   372→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   373→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   374→
   375→    return orphaned
   376→

   378→def move_cluster(
   379→    plan: PlanModel,
   380→    cluster_name: str,
   381→    position: str,
   382→    target: str | None = None,
   383→    offset: int | None = None,
   384→) -> int:
   385→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   386→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   387→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   388→    if cluster is None:
   389→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   390→
   391→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   392→    if not member_ids:
   393→        return 0
   394→
   395→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)

   398→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   399→# Focus
   400→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   401→
   402→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   403→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   404→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   405→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   406→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   407→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   408→
   409→
   410→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   411→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   412→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   413→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   414→
   415→
   416→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   417→# Reset
   418→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   419→
   420→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   421→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   422→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   423→    plan.clear()
   424→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan as _empty
   425→    for k, v in _empty().items():
   426→        plan[k] = v
   427→    plan["created"] = created
   428→
   429→
   430→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   431→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   432→
   433→    Cleans queue_order, deferred, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   434→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   435→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   436→    """
   437→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   438→    found = 0
   439→
   440→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   441→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   442→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   443→    for fid in finding_ids:
   444→        was_present = False
   445→        if fid in order:
   446→            order.remove(fid)
   447→            was_present = True
   448→        if fid in deferred:
   449→            deferred.remove(fid)
   450→            was_present = True
   451→        if fid in skipped:
   452→            skipped.pop(fid)
   453→            was_present = True
   454→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   455→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   456→            if fid in ids:
   457→                ids.remove(fid)
   458→                was_present = True
   459→        if was_present:
   460→            found += 1
   461→
   462→    return found
   463→

1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     9→    ALL_STATUSES,
    10→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    11→    build_subjective_items,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    15→    build_finding_items,
    16→    choose_fallback_tier,
    17→    group_queue_items,
    18→    item_explain,
    19→    item_sort_key,
    20→    tier_counts,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class QueueBuildOptions:
    27→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    28→
    29→    tier: int | None = None
    30→    count: int | None = 1
    31→    scan_path: str | None = None
    32→    scope: str | None = None
    33→    status: str = "open"
    34→    include_subjective: bool = True
    35→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    36→    chronic: bool = False
    37→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    38→    explain: bool = False
    39→    plan: dict | None = None
    40→    include_deferred: bool = False
    41→    cluster: str | None = None
    42→
    43→
    44→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    45→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    46→
    47→    items: list[dict]
    48→    total: int
    49→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    50→    requested_tier: int | None
    51→    selected_tier: int | None
    52→    fallback_reason: str | None
    53→    available_tiers: list[int]
    54→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    55→
    56→
    57→def _apply_plan_order(
    58→    items: list[dict],
    59→    plan: dict,
    60→    *,
    61→    include_deferred: bool = False,
    62→    cluster: str | None = None,
    63→) -> list[dict]:
    64→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    65→
    66→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    67→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    68→    3. Deferred items are appended last (or excluded).
    69→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    70→    """
    71→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    72→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    73→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys()) | set(plan.get("deferred", []))
    74→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    75→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    76→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    77→
    78→    # Build lookup
    79→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    80→    for item in items:
    81→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    82→
    83→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
    84→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    85→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    86→        if override.get("description"):
    87→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    88→        if override.get("note"):
    89→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    90→        if override.get("cluster"):
    91→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    92→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    93→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    94→                "name": cluster_name,
    95→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    96→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    97→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
    98→            }
    99→
   100→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   101→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   102→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   103→    for fid in queue_order:
   104→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   105→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   106→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   107→
   108→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   109→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   110→    for item in items:
   111→        item_id = item["id"]
   112→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   113→            continue
   114→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   115→            skipped_items.append(item)
   116→        else:
   117→            remaining.append(item)
   118→
   119→    # Assign queue positions
   120→    result = ordered + remaining
   121→    if include_deferred:
   122→        result = result + skipped_items
   123→
   124→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   125→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   126→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   127→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   128→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   129→            if skip_entry:
   130→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   131→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   132→                if skip_reason:
   133→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   134→            # Backwards compat
   135→            item["plan_deferred"] = True
   136→
   137→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   138→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   139→    if effective_cluster:
   140→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   141→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   142→        if cluster_member_ids:
   143→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   144→
   145→    return result
   146→
   147→
   148→def build_work_queue(
   149→    state: StateModel,
   150→    *,
   151→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   152→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   153→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   154→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   155→
   156→    status = resolved_options.status
   157→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   158→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   159→    try:
   160→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   161→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   162→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   163→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   164→
   165→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   166→        state,
   167→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   168→        status_filter=status,
   169→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   170→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   174→    if (
   175→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   176→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   177→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   178→    ):
   179→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   180→            state,
   181→            state.get("findings", {}),
   182→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   183→        )
   184→        for item in subjective_items:
   185→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   186→                all_items.append(item)
   187→
   188→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   189→
   190→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   191→    if resolved_options.plan:
   192→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   193→            all_items,
   194→            resolved_options.plan,
   195→            include_deferred=resolved_options.include_deferred,
   196→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   197→        )
   198→
   199→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   200→
   201→    requested_tier = (
   202→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   203→    )
   204→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   205→    fallback_reason = None
   206→    filtered = all_items
   207→
   208→    if requested_tier is not None:
   209→        filtered = [
   210→            item
   211→            for item in all_items
   212→            if int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == requested_tier
   213→        ]
   214→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   215→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   216→            if chosen is not None:
   217→                selected_tier = chosen
   218→                filtered = [
   219→                    item
   220→                    for item in all_items
   221→                    if int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == chosen
   222→                ]
   223→                fallback_reason = (
   224→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   225→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   226→                )
   227→        elif not filtered:
   228→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   229→
   230→    total = len(filtered)
   231→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   232→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   233→
   234→    if resolved_options.explain:
   235→        for item in filtered:
   236→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   237→
   238→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   239→    return {
   240→        "items": filtered,
   241→        "total": total,
   242→        "tier_counts": counts,
   243→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   244→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   245→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   246→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   247→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   248→    }
   249→
   250→
   251→__all__ = [
   252→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   253→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   254→    "WorkQueueResult",
   255→    "build_work_queue",
   256→    "group_queue_items",
   257→]
   258→

1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify resolve fixed "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   169→
   170→
   171→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   173→    if not dim_scores:
   174→        return {}
   175→
   176→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   177→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   178→    for entry in entries:
   179→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   180→        if not name:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   183→        [REDACTED](name)
   184→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   185→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   186→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   187→            if not key:
   188→                continue
   189→            aliases.add(key)
   190→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   191→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   192→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   193→        for alias in aliases:
   194→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   195→    return scores
   196→
   197→
   198→def build_subjective_items(
   199→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   200→) -> list[dict]:
   201→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   202→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   203→    if not dim_scores:
   204→        return []
   205→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   206→
   207→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   208→    if not subjective_entries:
   209→        return []
   210→    unassessed_dims = {
   211→        str(name).strip()
   212→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   213→            dim_scores
   214→        )
   215→    }
   216→
   217→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   218→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   219→    for finding in findings.values():
   220→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   221→            continue
   222→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   223→        if not dim_key:
   224→            continue
   225→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   226→
   227→    items: list[dict] = []
   228→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   229→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   230→        if not name:
   231→            continue
   232→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   233→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   234→            continue
   235→
   236→        [REDACTED](name)
   237→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   238→        cli_keys = [
   239→            str(key).strip().lower()
   240→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   241→            if str(key).strip()
   242→        ]
   243→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   244→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   245→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   246→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   247→            name in unassessed_dims
   248→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   249→        )
   250→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   251→        if is_unassessed:
   252→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   253→        elif is_stale:
   254→            if cli_keys:
   255→                primary_command = (
   256→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   257→                )
   258→            else:
   259→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   260→        else:
   261→            if open_review > 0:
   262→                primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   263→            elif cli_keys:
   264→                primary_command = (
   265→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   266→                )
   267→            else:
   268→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   269→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   270→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   271→        items.append(
   272→            {
   273→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   274→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   275→                "file": ".",
   276→                "tier": 4,
   277→                "effective_tier": 4,
   278→                "confidence": "medium",
   279→                "summary": summary,
   280→                "detail": {
   281→                    "dimension_name": name,
   282→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   283→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   284→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   285→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   286→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   287→                },
   288→                "status": "open",
   289→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   290→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   291→            }
   292→        )
   293→    return items
   294→

     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (

    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    84→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    85→        return (
    86→            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
    87→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items within T4.
    88→            subjective_score_value(item),
    89→            item.get("id", ""),
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    detail = detail_dict(item)
    93→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
    94→    return (
    95→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
    96→        0,
    97→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
    98→        -review_weight,
    99→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   100→        item.get("id", ""),
   101→    )
   102→
   103→
   104→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   105→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   106→
   107→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   108→        return {
   109→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   110→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   111→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   112→            "policy": (
   113→                "Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank "
   114→                "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   115→            ),
   116→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   117→        }
   118→
   119→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   120→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   121→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   122→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   123→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   124→    if is_subjective:
   125→        ranking_factors = ["tier fixed to T4", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   126→    elif is_review:
   127→        ranking_factors = [
   128→            "tier asc",
   129→            "confidence asc",
   130→            "review_weight desc",
   131→            "count desc",
   132→            "id asc",
   133→        ]
   134→    else:
   135→        ranking_factors = ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   136→    explain = {
   137→        "kind": "finding",
   138→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   139→        "confidence": confidence,
   140→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   141→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   142→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   143→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   144→    }
   145→    if is_review:
   146→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   147→    if is_subjective:
   148→        explain["policy"] = (
   149→            "Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank "
   150→            "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   151→        )
   152→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   153→    return explain
   154→
   155→
   156→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   157→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   158→    for item in items:
   159→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   160→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   161→    return counts
   162→
   163→
   164→def choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   165→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   166→    if not available:
   167→        return None
   168→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   169→
   170→
   171→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   172→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   173→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   174→    for item in items:
   175→        if group == "file":
   176→            key = item.get("file", "")
   177→        elif group == "detector":
   178→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   179→        elif group == "tier":
   180→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   181→        elif group == "cluster":
   182→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   183→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   184→        else:
   185→            key = "items"
   186→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   187→    return grouped
   188→
   189→
   190→__all__ = [
   191→    "build_finding_items",
   192→    "choose_fallback_tier",
   193→    "item_explain",
   194→    "item_sort_key",
   195→    "tier_counts",
   196→    "subjective_score_value",
   197→    "group_queue_items",
   198→]
   199→

    54→def _state_subjective_assessments(

    66→def _state_lang_capabilities(

    68→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
    69→    """Return normalized language capability map from state."""
    70→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    71→    if capabilities is None:
    72→        normalized: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
    73→        state["lang_capabilities"] = normalized
    74→        return normalized
    75→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict):

   143→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   144→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   145→    config: dict[str, object],
   146→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   147→    lang: LangRun | None,
   148→) -> LangRun | None:
   149→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   150→    if not lang:
   151→        return None
   152→
   153→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   154→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   155→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   156→        lang,
   157→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   158→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   159→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   160→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   161→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   162→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   163→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   164→        ),
   165→    )
   166→
   167→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   168→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   169→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   170→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   171→    }
   172→    return runtime_lang
   173→
   174→
   175→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   176→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   177→    *,
   178→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   179→) -> int:
   180→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   181→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   182→
   183→    reset_keys = {
   184→        key.strip()
   185→        for key in assessments
   186→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   187→    }
   188→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   189→
   190→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   191→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   192→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   193→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   194→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   195→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   196→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   197→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   198→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   199→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   200→            payload.pop("components", None)
   201→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   202→            continue
   203→        assessments[key] = {
   204→            "score": 0.0,
   205→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   206→            "assessed_at": now,
   207→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   208→            "placeholder": True,
   209→        }
   210→    return len(reset_keys)
   211→
   212→
   213→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   214→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   215→) -> int:
   216→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   217→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   218→
   219→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   220→    expired = 0
   221→    for payload in assessments.values():
   222→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   223→            continue
   224→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   225→            continue
   226→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   227→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   228→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   229→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   230→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   231→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   232→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   233→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   234→        payload.pop("components", None)
   235→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   236→        expired += 1
   237→    return expired

   240→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   241→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   242→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   243→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   244→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   245→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   246→    path = Path(args.path)
   247→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   248→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   249→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   250→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   251→        state
   252→    )
   253→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   254→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   255→            state,
   256→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   257→        )
   258→
   259→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   260→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   261→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   262→
   263→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   264→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   265→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   266→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   267→
   268→    return ScanRuntime(
   269→        args=args,
   270→        state_path=state_file,
   271→        state=state,
   272→        path=path,
   273→        config=config,
   274→        lang=lang,
   275→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   276→        profile=profile,
   277→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   278→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   279→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   280→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   281→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   282→    )
   283→
   284→
   285→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   286→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   287→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   288→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   289→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   290→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   291→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   292→        return findings
   293→
   294→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   295→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   296→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   297→    )
   298→    if not stale:
   299→        return findings
   300→
   301→    augmented = list(findings)
   302→    augmented.extend(stale)
   303→    for stale_finding in stale:
   304→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   305→    return augmented
   306→
   307→
   308→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   309→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   310→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   311→    *,
   312→    decay_scans: int,
   313→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   314→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   315→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   316→        findings,
   317→        state=runtime.state,
   318→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   319→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   320→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   321→    )
   322→
   323→
   324→def run_scan_generation(
   325→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   326→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   327→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   328→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import (
   329→        disable_parse_cache,
   330→        enable_parse_cache,
   331→    )
   332→
   333→    enable_file_cache()
   334→    enable_parse_cache()
   335→    try:
   336→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   337→            runtime.path,
   338→            lang=runtime.lang,
   339→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   340→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   341→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   342→                profile=runtime.profile,
   343→            ),
   344→        )
   345→    finally:
   346→        disable_parse_cache()
   347→        disable_file_cache()
   348→
   349→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   350→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   351→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   352→    )
   353→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   354→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   355→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   356→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   357→    )
   358→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   359→        findings,
   360→        runtime,
   361→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   362→    )
   363→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   364→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   365→
   366→
   367→def merge_scan_results(
   368→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   369→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   370→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   371→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   372→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   373→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   374→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   375→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   376→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   377→
   378→    if not path_changed:
   379→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   380→    else:
   381→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   382→    prev_dim_scores = (
   383→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   384→    )
   385→
   386→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   387→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   388→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   389→
   390→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   391→
   392→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   393→        runtime.state,
   394→        findings,
   395→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   396→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   397→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   398→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   399→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   400→            potentials=potentials,
   401→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   402→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   403→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   404→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   405→        ),
   406→    )
   407→
   408→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   409→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   410→    )
   411→    state_mod.save_state(
   412→        runtime.state,
   413→        runtime.state_path,
   414→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   415→    )
   416→
   417→    # Reconcile living plan after state save
   418→    try:
   419→        from desloppify.engine.plan import (
   420→            load_plan,
   421→            reconcile_plan_after_scan,
   422→            save_plan,
   423→        )
   424→
   425→        plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json"
   426→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   427→        if (
   428→            plan.get("queue_order")
   429→            or plan.get("overrides")
   430→            or plan.get("clusters")
   431→            or plan.get("skipped")
   432→            or plan.get("deferred")
   433→        ):
   434→            result = reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, runtime.state)
   435→            if result.changes:
   436→                save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   437→            if result.resurfaced:
   438→                print(colorize(
   439→                    f"  Plan: {len(result.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
   440→                    "cyan",
   441→                ))
   442→    except Exception:
   443→        pass  # Plan reconciliation is best-effort
   444→
   445→    return ScanMergeResult(
   446→        diff=diff,
   447→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   448→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   449→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   450→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   451→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   452→    )
   453→
   454→
   455→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   456→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   457→    config: dict[str, object],
   458→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   459→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   460→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   461→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   462→    )
   463→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   464→    open_findings = [
   465→        finding
   466→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   467→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   468→        ).values()
   469→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   470→    ]
   471→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   472→        open_findings,
   473→        budget=noise_budget,
   474→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   475→    )
   476→
   477→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   478→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   479→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   480→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   481→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   482→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   483→    )
   484→
   485→
   486→def persist_reminder_history(
   487→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   488→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   489→) -> None:
   490→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   491→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   492→        return
   493→
   494→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   495→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   496→    state_mod.save_state(
   497→        runtime.state,
   498→        runtime.state_path,
   499→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   500→    )
   501→
   502→
   503→__all__ = [
   504→    "ScanStateContractError",
   505→    "ScanMergeResult",
   506→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   507→    "ScanRuntime",
   508→    "merge_scan_results",
   509→    "persist_reminder_history",
   510→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   511→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   512→    "run_scan_generation",
   513→]
   514→
