```
1→"""Subjective scan reporting: common helpers, integrity checks, and output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify import scoring as scoring_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import coerce_target_score
     9→from desloppify.intelligence import integrity as subjective_integrity_mod
    10→
    11→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→# Common helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_common)
    13→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14→
    15→
    16→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    17→class SubjectiveFollowup:
    18→    threshold: float
    19→    threshold_label: str
    20→    low_assessed: list[dict]
    21→    rendered: str
    22→    command: str
    23→    integrity_notice: dict[str, object] | None
    24→    integrity_lines: list[tuple[str, str]]
    25→
    26→
    27→def flatten_cli_keys(items: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    28→    """Flatten CLI keys across up to max_items subjective entries, preserving order."""
    29→    seen: set[str] = set()
    30→    ordered: list[str] = []
    31→    for item in items[:max_items]:
    32→        for key in item.get("cli_keys", []):
    33→            if key in seen:
    34→                continue
    35→            ordered.append(key)
    36→            seen.add(key)
    37→    return ",".join(ordered)
    38→
    39→
    40→def render_subjective_scores(entries: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    41→    return ", ".join(
    42→        f"{entry.get('name', 'Subjective')} {float(entry.get('strict', entry.get('score', 100.0))):.1f}%"
    43→        for entry in entries[:max_items]
    44→    )
    45→
    46→
    47→def render_subjective_names(entries: list[dict], *, max_names: int = 3) -> str:
    48→    count = len(entries)
    49→    names = ", ".join(
    50→        str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")) for entry in entries[:max_names]
    51→    )
    52→    if count > max_names:
    53→        names = f"{names}, +{count - max_names} more"
    54→    return names
    55→
    56→
    57→def coerce_notice_count(value: object) -> int:
    58→    if isinstance(value, bool):
    59→        return 0
    60→    if isinstance(value, int):
    61→        return value
    62→    if isinstance(value, float):
    63→        return int(value)
    64→    if isinstance(value, str):
    65→        try:
    66→            return int(value.strip())
    67→        except ValueError:
    68→            return 0
    69→    return 0
    70→
    71→
    72→def coerce_str_keys(value: object) -> list[str]:
    73→    if not isinstance(value, list | tuple | set):
    74→        return []
    75→    return [key for key in value if isinstance(key, str) and key]
    76→
    77→
    78→def subjective_rerun_command(
    79→    items: list[dict],
    80→    *,
    81→    max_items: int = 5,
    82→    refresh: bool = True,
    83→    has_prior_review: bool | None = None,
    84→) -> str:
    85→    _ = refresh
    86→    dim_keys = flatten_cli_keys(items, max_items=max_items)
    87→
    88→    # If no evidence of prior runner usage, suggest --prepare first
    89→    if has_prior_review is False:
    90→        command_parts = ["desloppify", "review", "--prepare"]
    91→        if dim_keys:
    92→            command_parts.extend(["--dimensions", dim_keys])
    93→        cmd = f"`{' '.join(command_parts)}`"
    94→        return f"{cmd} (set up `--runner codex` for automated reviews)"
    95→
    96→    command_parts = [
    97→        "desloppify",
    98→        "review",
    99→        "--run-batches",
   100→        "--runner",
   101→        "codex",
   102→        "--parallel",
   103→        "--scan-after-import",
   104→    ]
   105→    if dim_keys:
   106→        command_parts.extend(["--dimensions", dim_keys])
   107→    return f"`{' '.join(command_parts)}`"
   108→
   109→
   110→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   111→# Integrity and dimension-mapping helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_integrity)
   112→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   113→
   114→
   115→def _subjective_display_name_from_key(dimension_key: str) -> str:
   116→    return scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.get(
   117→        dimension_key, dimension_key.replace("_", " ").title()
   118→    )
   119→
   120→
   121→def subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   122→    dimension_keys: list[str], entries: list[dict]
   123→) -> list[dict]:
   124→    by_key: dict[str, dict] = {}
   125→    for entry in entries:
   126→        for key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   127→            by_key.setdefault(str(key), entry)
   128→
   129→    mapped: list[dict] = []
   130→    for key in dimension_keys:
   131→        if key in by_key:
   132→            mapped.append(by_key[key])
   133→            continue
   134→        mapped.append(
   135→            {
   136→                "name": _subjective_display_name_from_key(key),
   137→                "score": 0.0,
   138→                "strict": 0.0,
   139→                "issues": 0,
   140→                "placeholder": False,
   141→                "cli_keys": [key],
   142→            }
   143→        )
   144→    return mapped
   145→
   146→
   147→def subjective_integrity_followup(
   148→    state: dict,
   149→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   150→    *,
   151→    threshold: float = 95.0,
   152→    max_items: int = 5,
   153→) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   154→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold, fallback=95.0)
   155→    raw_integrity_state = state.get("subjective_integrity")
   156→    integrity_state: dict[str, object] = (
   157→        raw_integrity_state if isinstance(raw_integrity_state, dict) else {}
   158→    )
   159→    status = str(integrity_state.get("status", "")).strip().lower()
   160→    raw_target = integrity_state.get("target_score")
   161→    target_display = coerce_target_score(raw_target, fallback=threshold_value)
   162→    matched_keys = coerce_str_keys(integrity_state.get("matched_dimensions", []))
   163→    reset_keys = coerce_str_keys(integrity_state.get("reset_dimensions", []))
   164→
   165→    if status == "penalized" and reset_keys:
   166→        reset_entries = subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   167→            reset_keys, subjective_entries
   168→        )
   169→        return {
   170→            "status": "penalized",
   171→            "count": len(reset_keys),
   172→            "target": target_display,
   173→            "entries": reset_entries,
   174→            "rendered": render_subjective_names(reset_entries),
   175→            "command": subjective_rerun_command(reset_entries, max_items=max_items),
   176→        }
   177→
   178→    if status == "warn" and matched_keys:
   179→        matched_entries = subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   180→            matched_keys, subjective_entries
   181→        )
   182→        return {
   183→            "status": "warn",
   184→            "count": len(matched_keys),
   185→            "target": target_display,
   186→            "entries": matched_entries,
   187→            "rendered": render_subjective_names(matched_entries),
   188→            "command": subjective_rerun_command(matched_entries, max_items=max_items),
   189→        }
   190→
   191→    at_target = sorted(
   192→        [
   193→            entry
   194→            for entry in subjective_entries
   195→            if not entry.get("placeholder")
   196→            and subjective_integrity_mod.matches_target_score(
   197→                float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))),
   198→                threshold_value,
   199→            )
   200→        ],
```

```
1→"""Post-scan narrative and integrity reporting."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→
     9→
    10→def _coerce_coverage_confidence(value: object, *, default: float = 1.0) -> float:
    11→    try:
    12→        parsed = float(value)
    13→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    14→        return default
    15→    return max(0.0, min(1.0, parsed))
    16→
    17→
    18→def _current_scan_coverage(state: dict, lang) -> dict:
    19→    scan_coverage = state.get("scan_coverage", {})
    20→    if not isinstance(scan_coverage, dict):
    21→        return {}
    22→    lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) if lang is not None else None
    23→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name:
    24→        entry = scan_coverage.get(lang_name, {})
    25→        return entry if isinstance(entry, dict) else {}
    26→    return {}
    27→
    28→
    29→def _coverage_reduction_warnings(state: dict, lang) -> list[str]:
    30→    coverage = _current_scan_coverage(state, lang)
    31→    detectors = coverage.get("detectors", {})
    32→    if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
    33→        return []
    34→
    35→    warnings: list[str] = []
    36→    for detector, payload in detectors.items():
    37→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    38→            continue
    39→        status = str(payload.get("status", "full")).strip().lower()
    40→        confidence = _coerce_coverage_confidence(payload.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
    41→        if status != "reduced" and confidence >= 1.0:
    42→            continue
    43→
    44→        summary = str(payload.get("summary", "")).strip()
    45→        impact = str(payload.get("impact", "")).strip()
    46→        remediation = str(payload.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    47→        detector_label = str(detector).strip() or "detector"
    48→
    49→        line = f"Coverage reduced ({detector_label}): {summary or 'reduced detector confidence.'}"
    50→        if impact:
    51→            line += f" Repercussion: {impact}"
    52→        if remediation:
    53→            line += f" Fix: {remediation}"
    54→        warnings.append(line)
    55→    return warnings
    56→
    57→
    58→def show_post_scan_analysis(
    59→    diff: dict,
    60→    state: dict,
    61→    lang,
    62→    *,
    63→    target_strict_score: float = 95.0,
    64→) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
    65→    """Print critical warnings + headline + pointers. Returns (warnings, narrative)."""
    66→    # Critical warnings only (reopened, cascading, chronic, coverage reduction)
    67→    warnings: list[str] = []
    68→    if diff["reopened"] > 5:
    69→        warnings.append(
    70→            f"{diff['reopened']} findings reopened — was a previous fix reverted?"
    71→        )
    72→    if diff["new"] > 10 and diff["auto_resolved"] < 3:
    73→        warnings.append(
    74→            f"{diff['new']} new findings with few resolutions — likely cascading."
    75→        )
    76→    chronic = diff.get("chronic_reopeners", [])
    77→    chronic_count = len(chronic) if isinstance(chronic, list) else chronic
    78→    if chronic_count > 0:
    79→        warnings.append(
    80→            f"⟳ {chronic_count} chronic reopener{'s' if chronic_count != 1 else ''} — "
    81→            "run `desloppify show --chronic` to see them."
    82→        )
    83→    warnings.extend(_coverage_reduction_warnings(state, lang))
    84→
    85→    for warning in warnings:
    86→        print(colorize(f"  {warning}", "yellow"))
    87→    if warnings:
    88→        print()
    89→
    90→    # Single narrative headline
    91→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    92→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    93→        state,
    94→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(
    95→            diff=diff,
    96→            lang=lang_name,
    97→            command="scan",
    98→        ),
    99→    )
   100→    if narrative.get("headline"):
   101→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['headline']}", "cyan"))
   102→
   103→    # Pointers — include plan reference when active
   104→    try:
   105→        from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan
   106→        _has_plan = has_living_plan()
   107→    except Exception:
   108→        _has_plan = False
   109→
   110→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` for the highest-priority item.", "dim"))
   111→    if _has_plan:
   112→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify plan` to see the updated living plan.", "dim"))
   113→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify status` for the full dashboard.", "dim"))
   114→    print()
   115→
   116→    return warnings, narrative
   117→
   118→
   119→def show_score_integrity(state: dict, diff: dict):
   120→    """Show Score Integrity section — surfaces wontfix debt and ignored findings."""
   121→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   122→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   123→    ignored = diff.get("ignored", 0)
   124→    ignore_patterns = diff.get("ignore_patterns", 0)
   125→    score_confidence = state.get("score_confidence", {})
   126→    confidence_reduced = (
   127→        isinstance(score_confidence, dict)
   128→        and str(score_confidence.get("status", "full")).lower() == "reduced"
   129→    )
   130→
   131→    if wontfix <= 5 and ignored <= 0 and ignore_patterns <= 0 and not confidence_reduced:
   132→        return
   133→
   134→    overall = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   135→    strict = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   136→    strict_gap = (
   137→        round(overall - strict, 1) if overall is not None and strict is not None else 0
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    # Wontfix % of actionable findings (open + wontfix + fixed + auto_resolved + false_positive)
   141→    actionable = (
   142→        stats.get("open", 0)
   143→        + wontfix
   144→        + stats.get("fixed", 0)
   145→        + stats.get("auto_resolved", 0)
   146→        + stats.get("false_positive", 0)
   147→    )
   148→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
   149→
   150→    print(colorize("  " + "┄" * 2 + " Score Integrity " + "┄" * 37, "dim"))
```

```
1→"""Canonical scorecard projection helpers shared across app/engine code."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import (
     8→    scorecard_dimension_rows,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→

    79→    subjective_display_names.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})

    84→        lowered_name = str(name).strip().lower()
    85→        is_subjective = (

    93→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
    94→        placeholder = bool(
    95→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
    96→            or (
    97→                data.get("score", 0) == 0

    99→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   100→            )
   101→        )

   103→        stale = bool(
   104→            dim_key
   105→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   106→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   107→        )
   108→        entries.append(
   109→            {
   110→                "name": name,
   111→                "score": score,
   112→                "strict": strict,
   113→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   114→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   115→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   116→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   117→                "stale": stale,
   118→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   119→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   120→            }
   121→        )
   122→    return entries
   123→
   124→
   125→def scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
   129→) -> list[dict]:

   134→    }

   137→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   138→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   139→        subjective = name in subjective_by_name
   140→        entry = {
   141→            "name": name,
   142→            "score": score,

   144→            "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   145→            "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   146→            "tier": int(

   148→            ),
   149→            "subjective": subjective,
   150→        }
   151→        if subjective:
   152→            sub = subjective_by_name[name]
   153→            entry["placeholder"] = bool(sub.get("placeholder"))
   154→            entry["cli_keys"] = list(sub.get("cli_keys", []))
   155→        payload.append(entry)
   156→    return payload
   157→
```

```
1→"""Scan merge/update operations for persisted findings state."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→__all__ = [
     9→    "MergeScanOptions",
    10→    "merge_scan",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.merge_findings import (
    14→    auto_resolve_disappeared,
    15→    find_suspect_detectors,
    16→    upsert_findings,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.merge_history import (
    19→    _append_scan_history,
    20→    _build_merge_diff,
    21→    _compute_suppression,
    22→    _merge_scan_inputs,
    23→    _record_scan_metadata,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    26→    ScanDiff,
    27→    StateModel,
    28→    ensure_state_defaults,
    29→    utc_now,
    30→    validate_state_invariants,
    31→)
    32→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    33→
    34→# Mechanical detectors → subjective dimensions they provide evidence for.
    35→# When findings from these detectors change during a scan, the corresponding
    36→# subjective assessments are marked stale so reviewers know to re-evaluate.
    37→_DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    38→    "structural": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    39→    "smells": ("design_coherence", "error_consistency"),
    40→    "global_mutable_config": ("initialization_coupling",),
    41→    "coupling": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    42→    "layer_violation": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    43→    "private_imports": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    44→    "dupes": ("convention_outlier",),
    45→    "boilerplate_duplication": ("convention_outlier",),
    46→    "naming": ("convention_outlier",),
    47→    "flat_dirs": ("package_organization",),
    48→    "orphaned": ("design_coherence",),
    49→    "uncalled_functions": ("design_coherence",),
    50→    "responsibility_cohesion": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    51→    "cycles": ("cross_module_architecture", "dependency_health"),
    52→}
    53→
    54→
    55→def _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
    56→    state: StateModel,
    57→    *,
    58→    changed_detectors: set[str],
    59→    now: str,
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings change.
    62→
    63→    Only marks dimensions that already have an assessment — doesn't create
    64→    new entries for dimensions that have never been reviewed.
    65→    """
    66→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    67→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    68→        return
    69→
    70→    affected_dims: set[str] = set()
    71→    for detector in changed_detectors:
    72→        dims = _DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS.get(detector, ())
    73→        affected_dims.update(dims)
    74→
    75→    if not affected_dims:
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    for dimension in sorted(affected_dims):
    79→        if dimension not in assessments:
    80→            continue
    81→        payload = assessments[dimension]
    82→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    83→            continue
    84→        # Don't overwrite if already stale
    85→        if payload.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    86→            continue
    87→        payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    88→        payload["refresh_reason"] = "mechanical_findings_changed"
    89→        payload["stale_since"] = now
    90→
    91→
    92→@dataclass
    93→class MergeScanOptions:
    94→    """Configuration bundle for merging a scan into persisted state."""
    95→
    96→    lang: str | None = None
    97→    scan_path: str | None = None
    98→    force_resolve: bool = False
    99→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = ()
   100→    potentials: dict[str, int] | None = None
   101→    merge_potentials: bool = False
   102→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   103→    include_slow: bool = True
   104→    ignore: list[str] | None = None
   105→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None
   106→
   107→
   108→def merge_scan(
   109→    state: StateModel,
   110→    current_findings: list[dict],
   111→    options: MergeScanOptions | None = None,
   112→) -> ScanDiff:
   113→    """Merge a fresh scan into existing state and return a diff summary."""
   114→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   115→    resolved_options = options or MergeScanOptions()
   116→
   117→    now = utc_now()
   118→    _record_scan_metadata(
   119→        state,
   120→        now,
   121→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   122→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   123→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   124→    )
   125→    _merge_scan_inputs(
   126→        state,
   127→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   128→        potentials=resolved_options.potentials,
   129→        merge_potentials=resolved_options.merge_potentials,
   130→        codebase_metrics=resolved_options.codebase_metrics,
   131→    )
   132→
   133→    existing = state["findings"]
   134→    ignore_patterns = (
   135→        resolved_options.ignore
   136→        if resolved_options.ignore is not None
   137→        else state.get("config", {}).get("ignore", [])
   138→    )
   139→    current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, current_by_detector, ignored_count, upsert_changed = (
   140→        upsert_findings(
   141→            existing,
   142→            current_findings,
   143→            ignore_patterns,
   144→            now,
   145→            lang=resolved_options.lang,
   146→        )
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    raw_findings = len(current_findings)
   150→    suppressed_pct = _compute_suppression(raw_findings, ignored_count)
   151→
   152→    ran_detectors = (
   153→        set(resolved_options.potentials.keys())
   154→        if resolved_options.potentials is not None
   155→        else None
   156→    )
   157→    suspect_detectors = find_suspect_detectors(
   158→        existing,
   159→        current_by_detector,
   160→        resolved_options.force_resolve,
   161→        ran_detectors,
   162→    )
   163→    auto_resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolve_changed = auto_resolve_disappeared(
   164→        existing,
   165→        current_ids,
   166→        suspect_detectors,
   167→        now,
   168→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   169→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   170→        exclude=resolved_options.exclude,
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    # Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings changed.
   174→    changed_detectors = upsert_changed | resolve_changed
   175→    if changed_detectors:
   176→        _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
   177→            state, changed_detectors=changed_detectors, now=now,
   178→        )
   179→
   180→    _recompute_stats(
   181→        state,
   182→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   183→        subjective_integrity_target=resolved_options.subjective_integrity_target,
   184→    )
   185→    _append_scan_history(
   186→        state,
   187→        now=now,
   188→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   189→        new_count=new_count,
   190→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   191→        ignored_count=ignored_count,
   192→        raw_findings=raw_findings,
   193→        suppressed_pct=suppressed_pct,
   194→        ignore_pattern_count=len(ignore_patterns),
   195→    )
   196→
   197→    chronic_reopeners = [
   198→        finding
   199→        for finding in existing.values()
   200→        if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2 and finding["status"] == "open"
   201→    ]
   202→
   203→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   204→    return _build_merge_diff(
   205→        new_count=new_count,
   206→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   207→        reopened_count=reopened_count,
   208→        current_ids=current_ids,
   209→        suspect_detectors=suspect_detectors,
   210→        chronic_reopeners=chronic_reopeners,
   211→        skipped_other_lang=skipped_other_lang,
   212→        skipped_out_of_scope=skipped_out_of_scope,
   213→        ignored_count=ignored_count,
   214→        ignore_pattern_count=len(ignore_patterns),
   215→        raw_findings=raw_findings,
   216→        suppressed_pct=suppressed_pct,
   217→    )
   218→
```

```
   186→def utc_now() -> str:
   187→    """Return current UTC timestamp with second-level precision."""
   188→    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   189→
   190→
   191→def empty_state() -> StateModel:
   192→    """Return a new empty state payload."""
   193→    return {
   194→        "version": CURRENT_VERSION,
   195→        "created": utc_now(),
   196→        "last_scan": None,
   197→        "scan_count": 0,
   198→        "overall_score": 0,
   199→        "objective_score": 0,
   200→        "strict_score": 0,
   201→        "verified_strict_score": 0,
   202→        "stats": {},
   203→        "findings": {},
   204→        "scan_coverage": {},
   205→        "score_confidence": {},
   206→        "subjective_integrity": {},
   207→        "subjective_assessments": {},
   208→    }
   209→
   210→
   211→def _as_non_negative_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:
   212→    try:
   213→        parsed = int(value)
   214→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   215→        return default
   216→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else 0
   217→
   218→
   219→def ensure_state_defaults(state: StateModel | dict) -> None:
   220→    """Normalize loose/legacy state payloads to a valid base shape in-place."""
   221→    for key, value in empty_state().items():
   222→        state.setdefault(key, value)
   223→
   224→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   225→        state["findings"] = {}
   226→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   227→        state["stats"] = {}
   228→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_history"), list):
   229→        state["scan_history"] = []
   230→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_coverage"), dict):
   231→        state["scan_coverage"] = {}
   232→    if not isinstance(state.get("score_confidence"), dict):
   233→        state["score_confidence"] = {}
   234→    if not isinstance(state.get("subjective_integrity"), dict):
   235→        state["subjective_integrity"] = {}
   236→
   237→    findings = state["findings"]
   238→    to_remove: list[str] = []
   239→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   240→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   241→            to_remove.append(finding_id)
   242→            continue
   243→
   244→        finding.setdefault("id", finding_id)
   245→        finding.setdefault("detector", "unknown")
   246→        finding.setdefault("file", "")
   247→        finding.setdefault("tier", 3)
   248→        finding.setdefault("confidence", "low")
   249→        finding.setdefault("summary", "")
   250→        finding.setdefault("detail", {})
   251→        finding.setdefault("status", "open")
   252→        finding["status"] = canonical_finding_status(
   253→            finding.get("status"),
   254→            default="open",
   255→        )
   256→        finding.setdefault("note", None)
   257→        finding.setdefault("first_seen", state.get("created") or utc_now())
   258→        finding.setdefault("last_seen", finding["first_seen"])
   259→        finding.setdefault("resolved_at", None)
   260→        finding["reopen_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(
   261→            finding.get("reopen_count", 0), default=0
   262→        )
   263→        finding.setdefault("suppressed", False)
   264→        finding.setdefault("suppressed_at", None)
   265→        finding.setdefault("suppression_pattern", None)
   266→
   267→    for finding_id in to_remove:
   268→        findings.pop(finding_id, None)
   269→
   270→    for entry in state["scan_history"]:
   271→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   272→            continue
   273→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
   274→        if integrity is not None and not isinstance(integrity, dict):
   275→            entry["subjective_integrity"] = None
   276→
   277→    state["scan_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(state.get("scan_count", 0), default=0)
   278→    return None
   279→
   280→
   281→def validate_state_invariants(state: StateModel) -> None:
   282→    """Raise ValueError when core state invariants are violated."""
   283→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   284→        raise ValueError("state.findings must be a dict")
   285→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   286→        raise ValueError("state.stats must be a dict")
   287→
   288→    findings = state["findings"]
   289→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   290→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   291→            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} must be a dict")
   292→        if finding.get("id") != finding_id:
   293→            raise ValueError(f"finding id mismatch for {finding_id!r}")
   294→        if finding.get("status") not in _ALLOWED_FINDING_STATUSES:
   295→            raise ValueError(
   296→                f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid status {finding.get('status')!r}"
   297→            )
   298→
   299→        tier = finding.get("tier")
   300→        if not isinstance(tier, int) or tier < 1 or tier > 4:
```

```
    55→    assert count == 1
```

```
1→"""Tests for shared queue selection in desloppify.work_queue."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     6→    QueueBuildOptions,
     7→)
     8→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     9→    build_work_queue as _build_work_queue,
    10→)
    11→
    12→
    13→def build_work_queue(state, **kwargs):
    14→    return _build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(**kwargs))
    15→
    16→
    17→def _finding(
    18→    fid: str,
    19→    *,
    20→    detector: str = "smells",
    21→    file: str = "src/a.py",
    22→    tier: int = 3,
    23→    confidence: str = "medium",
    24→    status: str = "open",
    25→    detail: dict | None = None,
    26→) -> dict:
    27→    return {
    28→        "id": fid,
    29→        "detector": detector,
    30→        "file": file,
    31→        "tier": tier,
    32→        "confidence": confidence,
    33→        "summary": fid,
    34→        "status": status,
    35→        "detail": detail or {},
    36→    }
    37→
    38→
    39→def _state(findings: list[dict], *, dimension_scores: dict | None = None) -> dict:
    40→    return {
    41→        "findings": {f["id"]: f for f in findings},
    42→        "dimension_scores": dimension_scores or {},
    43→    }
    44→
    45→
    46→def test_tier_fallback_selects_nearest_non_empty_tier():
    47→    state = _state(
    48→        [
    49→            _finding("t2_item", tier=2),
    50→            _finding("t4_item", tier=4),
    51→        ]
    52→    )
```

## Comprehensive Desloppify Plan System, Subjective Dimensions, and Work Queue Analysis

### 1. THE PLAN SYSTEM

#### 1.1 Plan Schema (PlanModel) - `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py`

The plan is a JSON file stored at `.desloppify/plan.json` with the following structure:

```python
class PlanModel(TypedDict):
    version: int              # PLAN_VERSION = 2
    created: str             # ISO timestamp
    updated: str             # ISO timestamp
    queue_order: list[str]   # Finding IDs in user-specified order
    deferred: list[str]      # Legacy list (kept for v1→v2 migration, always empty)
    skipped: dict            # Finding ID → SkipEntry
    active_cluster: str | None  # Currently focused cluster name
    overrides: dict          # Finding ID → ItemOverride (descriptions/notes)
    clusters: dict           # Cluster name → Cluster metadata
    superseded: dict         # Finding ID → SupersededEntry
```

**Key SkipEntry fields:**
- `finding_id`, `kind` (required) - "temporary", "permanent", or "false_positive"
- `reason`, `note`, `attestation` - optional metadata
- `review_after` - number of scans before re-surfacing (temporary only)
- `skipped_at_scan` - scan count when skipped (for review_after calculation)
- `created_at` - ISO timestamp

**Key ItemOverride fields:**
- `finding_id` (required)
- `description` - augmented description
- `note` - user annotation
- `cluster` - cluster membership reference
- `created_at`, `updated_at`

**Key Cluster fields:**
- `name` (required), `description`
- `finding_ids` - list of member IDs
- `created_at`, `updated_at`

**Key SupersededEntry fields:**
- `original_id`, `original_detector`, `original_file`, `original_summary` - original finding metadata
- `status` - "superseded", "remapped", or "dismissed"
- `superseded_at` - ISO timestamp
- `remapped_to` - optional remap target
- `candidates` - list of potential remaps (up to 5)
- `note` - preserved from override if exists

#### 1.2 How Items Initially Enter the Plan

**Items do NOT automatically enter `queue_order` on scan.** The plan is purely user-driven:

1. **Initial state**: When a plan.json is first created, `queue_order` is empty
2. **Adding items**: Users must manually call plan operations (via CLI subcommands or API):
   - `move_items()` - explicitly reorder/add items to queue
   - `unskip_items()` - bring items back from skipped → appended to queue_order
   - `resurface_stale_skips()` - automatic resurfacing of temporary skips past their `review_after` threshold

3. **Removing items**:
   - `skip_items()` - move to skipped dict
   - `defer_items()` (deprecated) - delegates to skip_items with kind="temporary"
   - `purge_ids()` - remove entirely from all plan structures (except overrides)

4. **Lifecycle example**:
   ```
   [No plan] → scan generates findings A, B, C (not in queue yet)
   → user runs: desloppify plan move "finding_a::" top
   → finding_a added to queue_order [0]
   → user skips finding_b: skip_items(plan, ["b"], kind="temporary", review_after=3, scan_count=5)
   → finding_b moves to skipped, not in queue_order
   → after 3+ scans (scan_count >= 8): resurface_stale_skips() moves b back to queue_order
   ```

#### 1.3 Persistence and Atomic Writes

- **Load**: `load_plan(path)` returns empty plan on missing/corrupted file
- **Validation**: `validate_plan()` checks queue_order/skipped don't overlap
- **Save**: `save_plan()` uses atomic write (temp file + os.replace) with `.json.bak` backup
- **Defaults**: `ensure_plan_defaults()` migrates v1→v2, normalizes all keys
- **Migration**: Old `deferred` list items auto-migrate to `skipped` with kind="temporary"

---

### 2. RECONCILIATION AFTER SCAN

#### 2.1 Reconciliation Flow - `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile.py`

**When**: Runs automatically after `merge_scan()` in `scan_workflow.py` (best-effort, no error propagation)

**What it does**:
```python
reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, state) → ReconcileResult
```

1. **Collect all referenced IDs** from plan:
   - queue_order, deferred, skipped, overrides, cluster finding_ids
   - Exclude already-superseded IDs

2. **Check each ID against state**:
   - If finding is missing or no longer open → move to superseded
   - Preserve any override note in superseded entry

3. **Resurface stale temporary skips**:
   - If `current_scan_count >= skipped_at_scan + review_after`
   - Remove from skipped, append to queue_order

4. **Prune old superseded entries**:
   - Remove entries older than 90 days (SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   - Also clean up stale overrides

**ReconcileResult** tracks:
- `superseded` - IDs moved to superseded
- `resurfaced` - IDs brought back from temporary skip
- `pruned` - IDs removed after TTL
- `changes` - total count

#### 2.2 When Plan is Empty

When the plan has no user intent (empty queue_order, deferred, overrides, clusters):
- `has_living_plan()` returns False
- No reconciliation runs (optimization)
- User sees unordered findings in default scan/next/status output
- Plan effectively dormant until first move/cluster/skip operation

---

### 3. SUBJECTIVE DIMENSIONS & STALENESS TRACKING

#### 3.1 What Makes a Dimension "Subjective"

**Definition**: A dimension that requires manual human assessment (unlike objective mechanics-based dimensions).

**Subjective dimensions tracked in state**:
- State path: `state["subjective_assessments"][dimension_key]` → `SubjectiveAssessment` TypedDict
- Examples: "design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness", "convention_outlier", "cross_module_architecture", "error_consistency", "initialization_coupling", "dependency_health", "package_organization"

**SubjectiveAssessment fields** (from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/schema.py`):
```python
class SubjectiveAssessment(TypedDict, total=False):
    score: float                          # 0-100
    integrity_penalty: str | None         # anti-gaming flag
    provisional_override: bool            # provisional score override
    provisional_until_scan: int           # scan threshold for provisional
    needs_review_refresh: bool            # TRUE = STALE
    refresh_reason: str | None            # e.g., "mechanical_findings_changed"
    stale_since: str | None               # ISO timestamp when marked stale
```

#### 3.2 How Staleness is Tracked

**Key field**: `needs_review_refresh` (boolean)

**Set to True when**:
- **Mechanical findings change** that provide evidence for the dimension
  - Detector map: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/merge.py`
  
  ```python
  _DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS = {
      "structural": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
      "smells": ("design_coherence", "error_consistency"),
      "coupling": ("cross_module_architecture",),
      "naming": ("convention_outlier",),
      # ... 12 detectors total
  }
  ```
  
  - During `merge_scan()`, if any detector's findings change (new/changed/auto-resolved), all mapped dimensions are marked stale
  - Function: `_mark_stale_on_mechanical_change()` in merge.py

**Detection pipeline**:
1. Scan runs, findings change
2. `_mark_stale_on_mechanical_change()` fires in merge_scan()
3. Sets `needs_review_refresh = True` + `refresh_reason = "mechanical_findings_changed"` + `stale_since = now`
4. On next scan reporting, `scorecard_subjective_entries()` checks:
   ```python
   stale = bool(
       dim_key
       and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
       and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   )
   ```
5. Stale dimensions show `[stale — re-review]` tag in scan output

#### 3.3 Refreshing Stale Dimensions

**What "refreshing" means**: User runs a review to reassess the dimension and update its score.

**How to refresh**:
```bash
desloppify review --prepare --dimensions <dim_key> [--runner codex]
```

**Result of refresh**:
- `needs_review_refresh` is cleared (set to False or removed)
- New score replaces old score
- Dimension re-enters mechanical scoring influence

#### 3.4 Subjective Work Items in Queue

**Synthetic work item creation** - `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py`

`build_subjective_items()` creates tier-4 synthetic items for subjective dimensions:
- Only created if `dimension_scores` exist and threshold filter met
- Marked below target threshold (strict < threshold, default 100%)
- Each item has:
  - `id` = "subjective::{dimension_slug}"
  - `detector` = "subjective_assessment"
  - `kind` = "subjective_dimension"
  - `tier` = 4 (always)
  - Summary includes score + `[stale — re-review]` tag if stale
  - Primary command varies by status (unassessed → `--prepare`, stale → `--prepare --dimensions`, etc.)

**Filter for inclusion**:
```python
strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
if strict_val >= threshold:
    continue  # Skip dimensions at/above threshold
```

#### 3.5 Placeholder vs Stale vs Unassessed

- **Placeholder**: `placeholder = True` → dimension never assessed (score = 0, brand new)
  - Shows `[unassessed]` tag
  - Primary command: `desloppify review --prepare`

- **Stale**: `needs_review_refresh = True` → dimension assessed before but mechanical evidence changed
  - Shows `[stale — re-review]` tag
  - Primary command: `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions <key>`

- **Unassessed by detection**: Very low/zero score with zero issues
  - Treated as placeholder
  - Triggers setup workflow

---

### 4. WORK QUEUE SYSTEM & PLAN INTERACTION

#### 4.1 Queue Building - `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`

**Main entry point**:
```python
build_work_queue(state, *, options=QueueBuildOptions) → WorkQueueResult
```

**Options include**:
- `plan` - dict with queue_order, skipped, overrides, clusters
- `include_deferred` - show skipped items last?
- `cluster` - filter to single cluster members
- `tier`, `count`, `scope`, `status` - filtering options
- `include_subjective` - add synthetic dimension items
- `subjective_threshold` - score threshold for dimension inclusion

#### 4.2 Queue Building Pipeline

**Step 1: Build mechanical findings** → `build_finding_items()`
- Filter by status, scope, path
- Skip suppressed findings
- Calculate effective_tier (subjective findings forced to T4)

**Step 2: Add subjective items** → `build_subjective_items()`
- Only if `include_subjective=True` and status in {"open", "all"} and not chronic
- Creates synthetic tier-4 items for dimensions below threshold

**Step 3: Sort all items** → `item_sort_key()`
- Primary: tier (ascending)
- Secondary: subjective (0=mechanical, 1=synthetic dimension) - mechanical sorts first
- Tertiary: confidence, count, id

**Step 4: Apply living plan order** → `_apply_plan_order()`
- If plan provided:
  1. Items in queue_order appear first in that order
  2. Remaining items keep mechanical sort
  3. Deferred/skipped items appear last (if include_deferred=True)
  4. Annotate each item with plan metadata (cluster, note, description)
  5. Filter to cluster if active_cluster set

**Step 5: Tier selection & filtering**
- If specific tier requested: filter to that tier
- If no items in tier: fallback to nearest non-empty tier (unless no_tier_fallback=True)
- Limit to count (default 1)

#### 4.3 Item Annotation with Plan Metadata

During `_apply_plan_order()`, items get enriched with:
- `plan_description` - from override
- `plan_note` - from override
- `plan_cluster` - cluster metadata + sibling_ids
- `plan_deferred` - boolean if in skipped (backwards compat)
- `plan_skipped` - boolean if in skipped (new)
- `plan_skip_kind` - "temporary"/"permanent"/"false_positive"
- `plan_skip_reason` - from skip entry
- `queue_position` - 1-indexed position in result list

#### 4.4 Subjective vs Mechanical Ranking

**Within tier 4**:
- Subjective dimension items sort AFTER mechanical T4 findings
- Sort key second element: 0 (mechanical) vs 1 (synthetic dimension)
- Reason: Mechanical T4 issues take priority over subjective dimension coaching

**Effective tier mapping**:
- Mechanical subjective findings (detector="review" or "holistic_review"): use natural tier (T1-T3)
- Synthetic subjective items: always T4

---

### 5. EMPTY PLAN BEHAVIOR

**When plan is empty**:
- `has_living_plan()` returns False (no queue_order, overrides, clusters, skipped, deferred)
- No plan reconciliation after scan
- `build_work_queue()` without plan parameter uses pure mechanical ranking
- Subjective items still included if threshold met
- No reordering by queue_order
- No cluster focusing
- Each scan starts fresh queue ranking

**User experience**:
- Scan shows findings in default order (T1→T4, confidence, count)
- `desloppify next` shows single highest-priority mechanical + subjective item
- `desloppify plan` shows empty template waiting for first user intent
- No resurfacing of temporarily skipped items (reconciliation skipped)

---

### 6. KEY DATA FLOW SUMMARY

```
Scan runs:
  ├─ generate_findings()
  ├─ merge_scan(state, findings)
  │  ├─ upsert_findings()
  │  ├─ _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change() if detectors changed
  │  │  └─ Sets needs_review_refresh=True on affected dimensions
  │  └─ _recompute_stats()
  ├─ save_state()
  └─ reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, state)
     ├─ Move disappeared IDs to superseded
     ├─ Resurface stale skips if review_after threshold met
     └─ Prune old superseded entries

Command (next/show/status):
  ├─ build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(plan=...))
  │  ├─ build_finding_items()
  │  ├─ build_subjective_items()
  │  ├─ item_sort_key() sorting
  │  └─ _apply_plan_order() if plan provided
  │     ├─ Reorder by queue_order
  │     ├─ Annotate with overrides/cluster/skip metadata
  │     └─ Filter to cluster if active
  └─ Render ranked items
```

```
1→"""Preflight guards for subjective review reruns.
     2→
     3→Blocks --prepare / --run-batches / --external-start when the objective plan
     4→still has open items, unless --force-review-rerun is set.  Also auto-clears
     5→stale subjective markers before a new review cycle.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import sys
    11→
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    14→from desloppify.state import StateModel, save_state
    15→
    16→from .helpers import parse_dimensions
    17→
    18→
    19→def clear_stale_subjective_entries(
    20→    state: StateModel,
    21→    *,
    22→    dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    23→) -> list[str]:
    24→    """Clear ``needs_review_refresh`` markers from subjective assessments.
    25→
    26→    When *dimensions* is provided, only those dimensions are cleared;
    27→    otherwise all stale dimensions are cleared.
    28→
    29→    Returns the list of dimension keys that were cleared.
    30→    """
    31→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    32→    cleared: list[str] = []
    33→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    34→        if not isinstance(assessment, dict):
    35→            continue
    36→        if dimensions is not None and dim_key not in dimensions:
    37→            continue
    38→        if assessment.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    39→            assessment.pop("needs_review_refresh", None)
    40→            assessment.pop("stale_since", None)
    41→            assessment.pop("refresh_reason", None)
    42→            cleared.append(dim_key)
    43→    return cleared
    44→
    45→
    46→def _scored_dimensions(state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
    47→    """Return dimension keys that already have a nonzero subjective score."""
    48→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    49→    scored: list[str] = []
    50→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    51→        if isinstance(assessment, dict):
    52→            if assessment.get("score", 0):
    53→                scored.append(dim_key)
    54→        elif isinstance(assessment, (int, float)) and assessment:
    55→            scored.append(dim_key)
    56→    return sorted(scored)
    57→
    58→
    59→def review_rerun_preflight(
    60→    state: StateModel,
    61→    args,
    62→    *,
    63→    state_file=None,
    64→    save_fn=save_state,
    65→) -> None:
    66→    """Single entry point: gate check -> clear stale -> save.
    67→
    68→    Exits with code 1 when open objective items exist and
    69→    ``--force-review-rerun`` is not set.  On success, clears stale
    70→    subjective markers for the targeted dimensions and persists state.
    71→    """
    72→    dimensions = parse_dimensions(args)
    73→
    74→    # --force-review-rerun bypasses the gate
    75→    if getattr(args, "force_review_rerun", False):
    76→        print(
    77→            colorize(
    78→                "  --force-review-rerun: bypassing objective-plan-drained check.",
    79→                "yellow",
    80→            )
    81→        )
    82→    else:
    83→        # Only gate dimensions that are actually targeted by this review run.
    84→        scored_dims = _scored_dimensions(state)
    85→        if dimensions is not None:
    86→            blocking_dims = sorted(d for d in scored_dims if d in dimensions)
    87→        else:
    88→            blocking_dims = scored_dims
    89→
    90→        # No gate when none of the targeted dimensions have prior scores —
    91→        # this is a first run for these dimensions, not a rerun.
    92→        if blocking_dims:
    93→            result = build_work_queue(
    94→                state,
    95→                options=QueueBuildOptions(
    96→                    status="open",
    97→                    include_subjective=False,
    98→                    count=None,
    99→                ),
   100→            )
   101→            total = result["total"]
   102→            if total > 0:
   103→                print(
   104→                    colorize(
   105→                        f"  Blocked: {total} open objective finding(s) — this is a review rerun.",
   106→                        "red",
   107→                    ),
   108→                    file=sys.stderr,
   109→                )
   110→                print(
   111→                    colorize(
   112→                        f"  Scored dimensions: {', '.join(blocking_dims)}",
   113→                        "yellow",
   114→                    ),
   115→                    file=sys.stderr,
   116→                )
   117→                if dimensions is not None:
   118→                    unscored = sorted(dimensions - set(blocking_dims))
   119→                    if unscored:
   120→                        print(
   121→                            f"  Tip: target only unscored dimensions with "
   122→                            f"--dimensions {','.join(unscored)}",
   123→                            file=sys.stderr,
   124→                        )
   125→                print(
   126→                    "  Resolve open items first, or override with --force-review-rerun",
   127→                    file=sys.stderr,
   128→                )
   129→                sys.exit(1)
   130→
   131→    # Gate passed — clear stale for targeted dims
   132→    cleared = clear_stale_subjective_entries(state, dimensions=dimensions)
   133→    if cleared and state_file:
   134→        save_fn(state, state_file)
   135→        print(
   136→            colorize(
   137→                f"  Cleared stale review markers: {', '.join(sorted(cleared))}",
   138→                "cyan",
   139→            )
   140→        )
   141→
```

```
1→"""CLI entrypoint for review command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→
    12→from .batch import _do_run_batches
    13→from .external import do_external_start, do_external_submit
    14→from .import_cmd import do_import, do_validate_import
    15→from .preflight import review_rerun_preflight
    16→from .prepare import do_prepare
    17→
    18→
    19→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
    20→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
    21→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
    22→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
    23→        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
    24→        if not callable(reconfigure):
    25→            continue
    26→        try:
    27→            reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
    28→        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
    29→            continue
    30→
    31→
    32→def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    33→    """Prepare or import subjective code review findings."""
    34→    _enable_live_review_output()
    35→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    36→    state_file = runtime.state_path
    37→    state = runtime.state
    38→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    39→
    40→    if not lang:
    41→        print(
    42→            colorize("  Error: could not detect language. Use --lang.", "red"),
    43→            file=sys.stderr,
    44→        )
    45→        sys.exit(1)
    46→
    47→    merge = bool(getattr(args, "merge", False))
    48→    run_batches = bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False))
    49→    external_start = bool(getattr(args, "external_start", False))
    50→    external_submit = bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False))
    51→    import_file = getattr(args, "import_file", None)
    52→    validate_import_file = getattr(args, "validate_import_file", None)
    53→
    54→    import_mode = bool(import_file) and not external_submit
    55→    mode_flags = [
    56→        merge,
    57→        run_batches,
    58→        external_start,
    59→        external_submit,
    60→        import_mode,
    61→        bool(validate_import_file),
    62→    ]
    63→    if sum(1 for enabled in mode_flags if enabled) > 1:
    64→        print(
    65→            colorize(
    66→                "  Error: choose one review mode per command "
    67→                "(--merge | --run-batches | --external-start | --external-submit | --import | --validate-import).",
    68→                "red",
    69→            ),
    70→            file=sys.stderr,
    71→        )
    72→        sys.exit(1)
    73→
    74→    if external_submit and not import_file:
    75→        print(
    76→            colorize(
    77→                "  Error: --external-submit requires --import FILE.",
    78→                "red",
    79→            ),
    80→            file=sys.stderr,
    81→        )
    82→        sys.exit(2)
    83→
    84→    if external_submit and not getattr(args, "session_id", None):
    85→        print(
    86→            colorize(
    87→                "  Error: --external-submit requires --session-id.",
    88→                "red",
    89→            ),
    90→            file=sys.stderr,
    91→        )
    92→        sys.exit(2)
    93→
    94→    if merge:
    95→        from desloppify.app.commands.review.merge import do_merge
    96→
    97→        do_merge(args)
    98→        return
    99→
   100→    if run_batches:
   101→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   102→        _do_run_batches(
   103→            args,
   104→            state,
   105→            lang,
   106→            state_file,
   107→            config=runtime.config,
   108→        )
   109→        return
   110→
   111→    if external_start:
   112→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   113→        do_external_start(
   114→            args,
   115→            state,
   116→            lang,
   117→            config=runtime.config,
   118→        )
   119→        return
   120→
   121→    if external_submit:
   122→        do_external_submit(
   123→            import_file=str(import_file),
   124→            session_id=str(getattr(args, "session_id")),
   125→            state=state,
   126→            lang=lang,
   127→            state_file=state_file,
   128→            config=runtime.config,
   129→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
   130→            scan_after_import=bool(getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False)),
   131→            scan_path=str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."),
   132→            dry_run=bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False)),
   133→        )
   134→        return
   135→
   136→    if validate_import_file:
   137→        do_validate_import(
   138→            validate_import_file,
   139→            lang,
   140→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
   141→            manual_override=bool(getattr(args, "manual_override", False)),
   142→            attested_external=bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False)),
   143→            manual_attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),
   144→        )
   145→        return
   146→
   147→    if import_file:
   148→        do_import(
   149→            import_file,
   150→            state,
   151→            lang,
   152→            state_file,
   153→            config=runtime.config,
   154→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
   155→            manual_override=bool(getattr(args, "manual_override", False)),
   156→            attested_external=bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False)),
   157→            manual_attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),
   158→        )
   159→    else:
   160→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   161→        do_prepare(args, state, lang, state_file, config=runtime.config)
   162→
```

```
    17→def _make_args(**overrides) -> SimpleNamespace:
    18→    defaults = {"force_review_rerun": False, "dimensions": None}
    19→    defaults.update(overrides)
    20→    return SimpleNamespace(**defaults)
    21→
    22→
    23→def _state_with_prior_review() -> dict:
    24→    """State that has a prior subjective review (nonzero dimension scores)."""
    25→    return {
    26→        "subjective_assessments": {

    28→            "logic_clarity": {"score": 90.0},

    39→def _wq_result(items: list[dict]) -> dict:
    40→    return {
    41→        "items": items,
    42→        "total": len(items),
    43→        "tier_counts": {},
    44→        "requested_tier": None,

    52→def test_blocked_when_open_objective_items(capsys):
    53→    """Preflight blocks when open objective findings exist."""
    54→    state = _state_with_prior_review()
    55→    items = [
    56→        {"id": f"f{i}", "summary": f"Finding {i}"} for i in range(3)
    57→    ]
    58→    with patch(_BUILD_WQ, return_value=_wq_result(items)):
    59→        with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as exc:
    60→            review_rerun_preflight(state, _make_args())
    61→        assert exc.value.code == 1
    62→
    63→    err = capsys.readouterr().err
    64→    assert "3 open objective finding(s)" in err
    65→    assert "--force-review-rerun" in err
    66→
    67→

   410→# -- Mode gating via mock-based dispatch (entrypoint) --------------------------
   411→#
   412→# These tests call cmd_review with patched dependencies and verify that
   413→# review_rerun_preflight is (or is not) invoked depending on the mode.
   414→
   415→
   416→_PREFLIGHT = "desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint.review_rerun_preflight"
   417→_RUNTIME = "desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint.command_runtime"
   418→_LANG = "desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint.resolve_lang"
   419→
   420→
   421→def _review_args(**overrides) -> SimpleNamespace:
   422→    """Build a minimal review args namespace for cmd_review."""
   423→    defaults = {
   424→        "path": ".",
   425→        "lang": "python",
   426→        "force_review_rerun": False,
   427→        "dimensions": None,
   428→        "merge": False,
   429→        "run_batches": False,
   430→        "external_start": False,
   431→        "external_submit": False,
   432→        "import_file": None,
   433→        "validate_import_file": None,
   434→        "session_id": None,
   435→    }
   436→    defaults.update(overrides)
   437→    return SimpleNamespace(**defaults)
   438→
   439→
   440→def _mock_runtime():
   441→    """Return a mock command_runtime result."""
   442→    rt = MagicMock()
   443→    rt.state_path = "/tmp/state.json"
   444→    rt.state = {}
   445→    rt.config = {}
   446→    return rt
   447→
   448→
   449→def _gating_patches():
   450→    """Return shared patches for runtime + lang."""
   451→    return {
   452→        "runtime": patch(_RUNTIME, return_value=_mock_runtime()),
   453→        "lang": patch(_LANG, return_value=MagicMock(name="python")),
   454→    }
   455→
   456→
   457→def test_prepare_calls_preflight():
   458→    """Default (prepare) mode invokes preflight."""
   459→    from desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint import cmd_review
   460→
   461→    with (
   462→        patch(_PREFLIGHT) as mock_pf,
   463→        patch(_RUNTIME, return_value=_mock_runtime()),
   464→        patch(_LANG, return_value=MagicMock(name="python")),
   465→        patch("desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint.do_prepare"),
   466→    ):
   467→        cmd_review(_review_args())
   468→        mock_pf.assert_called_once()
   469→
   470→
   471→def test_run_batches_calls_preflight():
   472→    """--run-batches mode invokes preflight."""
   473→    from desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint import cmd_review
   474→
   475→    with (
   476→        patch(_PREFLIGHT) as mock_pf,
   477→        patch(_RUNTIME, return_value=_mock_runtime()),
   478→        patch(_LANG, return_value=MagicMock(name="python")),
   479→        patch("desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint._do_run_batches"),
   480→    ):
   481→        cmd_review(_review_args(run_batches=True))
   482→        mock_pf.assert_called_once()

   484→
```

```
1→"""Action computation engine used by narrative query generation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import DETECTOR_TOOLS
     9→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.action_models import ActionContext, ActionItem
    10→from desloppify.languages import get_lang
    11→from desloppify.scoring import (
    12→    compute_score_impact,
    13→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    14→    merge_potentials,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    17→
    18→
    19→def supported_fixers(state: StateModel, lang: str | None) -> set[str] | None:
    20→    """Return supported fixers for the active language, or None when unknown."""
    21→    if not lang:
    22→        return None
    23→
    24→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities", {}).get(lang, {})
    25→    fixers = capabilities.get("fixers")
    26→    if isinstance(fixers, list):
    27→        return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
    28→
    29→    try:
    30→        return set(get_lang(lang).fixers.keys())
    31→    except (ImportError, ValueError):
    32→        return None
    33→
    34→
    35→def _impact_calculator(
    36→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    37→    state: StateModel,
    38→) -> Callable[[str, int], float]:
    39→    """Build an impact estimator closure keyed by detector and count."""
    40→    merged_potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
    41→    if not merged_potentials or not dimension_scores:
    42→        return lambda _detector, _count: 0.0
    43→
    44→    scoring_view = {
    45→        name: {
    46→            "score": values["score"],
    47→            "tier": values.get("tier", 3),
    48→            "detectors": values.get("detectors", {}),
    49→        }
    50→        for name, values in dimension_scores.items()
    51→    }
    52→
    53→    def _impact(detector: str, count: int) -> float:
    54→        return compute_score_impact(
    55→            scoring_view, merged_potentials, detector, count
    56→        )
    57→
    58→    return _impact
    59→
    60→
    61→def _dimension_name(detector: str) -> str:
    62→    """Resolve user-facing dimension name for a detector."""
    63→    dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    64→    return dimension.name if dimension else "Unknown"
    65→
    66→
    67→def _fixer_has_applicable_findings(
    68→    state: StateModel, detector: str, fixer_name: str
    69→) -> bool:
    70→    """For the smells detector, verify the fixer has matching open findings.
    71→
    72→    The smells detector aggregates many smell types but each fixer only handles
    73→    one sub-type (e.g. ``dead-useeffect`` only fixes ``dead_useeffect`` smells).
    74→    Without this check, a React-specific fixer can be suggested to projects that
    75→    have no React code at all, producing a confusing "Found 0 candidates" result.
    76→
    77→    For all other detectors the fixer is considered universally applicable.
    78→    """
    79→    if detector != "smells":
    80→        return True
    81→    smell_id = fixer_name.replace("-", "_")
    82→    return any(
    83→        f.get("status") == "open"
    84→        and not f.get("suppressed")
    85→        and f.get("detector") == "smells"
    86→        and f.get("detail", {}).get("smell_id") == smell_id
    87→        for f in state.get("findings", {}).values()
    88→    )
    89→
    90→
    91→def _append_auto_fix_actions(
    92→    actions: list[ActionItem],
    93→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
    94→    supported: set[str] | None,
    95→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
    96→    state: StateModel,
    97→) -> None:
    98→    """Append auto-fix/manual-fix actions for detectors with auto fixers."""
    99→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
   100→        if tool_info["action_type"] != "auto_fix":
   101→            continue
   102→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
   103→        if count == 0:
   104→            continue
   105→
   106→        impact = round(impact_for(detector, count), 1)
   107→        available_fixers = [
   108→            fixer
   109→            for fixer in tool_info["fixers"]
   110→            if (supported is None or fixer in supported)
   111→            and _fixer_has_applicable_findings(state, detector, fixer)
   112→        ]
   113→        if not available_fixers:
   114→            actions.append(
   115→                {
   116→                    "type": "manual_fix",
   117→                    "detector": detector,
   118→                    "count": count,
   119→                    "description": (
   120→                        f"{count} {detector} findings — inspect with "
   121→                        f"`desloppify next` and fix manually"
   122→                    ),
   123→                    "command": f"desloppify show {detector} --status open",
   124→                    "impact": impact,
   125→                    "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   126→                }
   127→            )
   128→            continue
   129→
   130→        fixer = available_fixers[0]
   131→        actions.append(
   132→            {
   133→                "type": "auto_fix",
   134→                "detector": detector,
   135→                "count": count,
   136→                "description": (
   137→                    f"{count} {detector} findings — run "
   138→                    f"`desloppify fix {fixer} --dry-run` to preview, then apply"
   139→                ),
   140→                "command": f"desloppify fix {fixer} --dry-run",
   141→                "impact": impact,
   142→                "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   143→            }
   144→        )
   145→
   146→
   147→def _append_reorganize_actions(
   148→    actions: list[ActionItem],
   149→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
   150→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
   151→) -> None:
   152→    """Append structure/move oriented actions."""
   153→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
   154→        if tool_info["action_type"] != "reorganize":
   155→            continue
   156→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
   157→        if count == 0:
   158→            continue
   159→
   160→        guidance = tool_info.get("guidance", "restructure with move")
   161→        actions.append(
   162→            {
   163→                "type": "reorganize",
   164→                "detector": detector,
   165→                "count": count,
   166→                "description": f"{count} {detector} findings — {guidance}",
   167→                "command": f"desloppify show {detector} --status open",
   168→                "impact": round(impact_for(detector, count), 1),
   169→                "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   170→            }
   171→        )
   172→
   173→
   174→def _build_refactor_entry(
   175→    detector: str,
   176→    tool_info: dict[str, Any],
   177→    count: int,
   178→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
   179→) -> ActionItem:
   180→    """Build one refactor/manual action row."""
   181→    guidance = tool_info.get("guidance", "manual fix")
   182→    adjusted_info = {**tool_info, "guidance": guidance}
   183→
   184→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   185→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   186→        description = f"{count} files need design review — run holistic review to refresh subjective scores"
   187→    elif detector == "review":
   188→        command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   189→        suffix = "s" if count != 1 else ""
   190→        description = (
   191→            f"{count} review finding{suffix} need investigation — "
   192→            "run `desloppify show review --status open` to see them"
   193→        )
   194→        adjusted_info = {**adjusted_info, "action_type": "refactor"}
   195→    else:
   196→        command = f"desloppify show {detector} --status open"
   197→        description = f"{count} {detector} findings — {guidance}"
   198→
   199→    return {
   200→        "type": adjusted_info["action_type"],
   201→        "detector": detector,
   202→        "count": count,
   203→        "description": description,
   204→        "command": command,
   205→        "impact": round(impact_for(detector, count), 1),
   206→        "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   207→    }
   208→
   209→
   210→def _append_refactor_actions(
   211→    actions: list[ActionItem],
   212→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
   213→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
   214→) -> None:
   215→    """Append refactor/manual actions after auto-fix/reorg buckets."""
   216→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
   217→        if tool_info["action_type"] not in {"refactor", "manual_fix"}:
   218→            continue
   219→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
   220→        if count == 0:
   221→            continue
   222→        actions.append(_build_refactor_entry(detector, tool_info, count, impact_for))
   223→
   224→
   225→def _append_debt_action(actions: list[ActionItem], debt: dict[str, float]) -> None:
   226→    """Append wontfix-debt callout when gap is material."""
   227→    gap = float(debt.get("overall_gap", 0.0) or 0.0)
   228→    if gap <= 2.0:
   229→        return
   230→    actions.append(
   231→        {
   232→            "type": "debt_review",
   233→            "detector": None,
   234→            "description": f"{gap} pts of wontfix debt — review stale decisions",
   235→            "command": "desloppify show --status wontfix",
   236→            "gap": gap,
   237→        }
   238→    )
   239→
   240→
   241→def _assign_priorities(actions: list[ActionItem]) -> list[ActionItem]:
   242→    """Sort and assign sequential priorities."""
   243→    type_order = {
   244→        "issue_queue": 0,
   245→        "auto_fix": 1,
   246→        "reorganize": 2,
   247→        "refactor": 3,
   248→        "manual_fix": 4,
   249→        "debt_review": 5,
   250→    }
   251→    actions.sort(
   252→        key=lambda action: (type_order.get(action["type"], 9), -action.get("impact", 0))
   253→    )
   254→    for index, action in enumerate(actions, start=1):
   255→        action["priority"] = index
   256→    return actions
   257→
   258→
   259→def compute_actions(ctx: ActionContext) -> list[ActionItem]:
   260→    """Compute prioritized action list with tool mapping."""
   261→    actions: list[ActionItem] = []
   262→    impact_for = _impact_calculator(ctx.dimension_scores, ctx.state)
   263→    supported = supported_fixers(ctx.state, ctx.lang)
   264→
   265→    _append_auto_fix_actions(actions, ctx.by_detector, supported, impact_for, ctx.state)
   266→    _append_reorganize_actions(actions, ctx.by_detector, impact_for)
   267→    _append_refactor_actions(actions, ctx.by_detector, impact_for)
   268→    _append_debt_action(actions, ctx.debt)
   269→
   270→    return _assign_priorities(actions)
   271→
```
