```
    34→# Mechanical detectors → subjective dimensions they provide evidence for.
    35→# When findings from these detectors change during a scan, the corresponding
    36→# subjective assessments are marked stale so reviewers know to re-evaluate.
    37→_DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    38→    "structural": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    39→    "smells": ("design_coherence", "error_consistency"),
    40→    "global_mutable_config": ("initialization_coupling",),
    41→    "coupling": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    42→    "layer_violation": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    43→    "private_imports": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    44→    "dupes": ("convention_outlier",),
    45→    "boilerplate_duplication": ("convention_outlier",),
    46→    "naming": ("convention_outlier",),
    47→    "flat_dirs": ("package_organization",),
    48→    "orphaned": ("design_coherence",),
    49→    "uncalled_functions": ("design_coherence",),
    50→    "responsibility_cohesion": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    51→    "cycles": ("cross_module_architecture", "dependency_health"),
    52→}
    53→
    54→
    55→def _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
    56→    state: StateModel,
    57→    *,
    58→    changed_detectors: set[str],
    59→    now: str,
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings change.
    62→
    63→    Only marks dimensions that already have an assessment — doesn't create
    64→    new entries for dimensions that have never been reviewed.
    65→    """
    66→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    67→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    68→        return
    69→
    70→    affected_dims: set[str] = set()
    71→    for detector in changed_detectors:
    72→        dims = _DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS.get(detector, ())
    73→        affected_dims.update(dims)
    74→
    75→    if not affected_dims:
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    for dimension in sorted(affected_dims):
    79→        if dimension not in assessments:
    80→            continue
    81→        payload = assessments[dimension]
    82→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    83→            continue
    84→        # Don't overwrite if already stale
    85→        if payload.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    86→            continue
    87→        payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    88→        payload["refresh_reason"] = "mechanical_findings_changed"
    89→        payload["stale_since"] = now
    90→
    91→
    92→@dataclass
    93→class MergeScanOptions:
    94→    """Configuration bundle for merging a scan into persisted state."""
    95→
    96→    lang: str | None = None
    97→    scan_path: str | None = None
    98→    force_resolve: bool = False
    99→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = ()
   100→    potentials: dict[str, int] | None = None
   101→    merge_potentials: bool = False
   102→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   103→    include_slow: bool = True
   104→    ignore: list[str] | None = None
   105→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None
   106→
   107→
   108→def merge_scan(
   109→    state: StateModel,
   110→    current_findings: list[dict],
   111→    options: MergeScanOptions | None = None,
   112→) -> ScanDiff:
   113→    """Merge a fresh scan into existing state and return a diff summary."""
   114→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   115→    resolved_options = options or MergeScanOptions()
   116→
   117→    now = utc_now()
   118→    _record_scan_metadata(
   119→        state,
   120→        now,
   121→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   122→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   123→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   124→    )
   125→    _merge_scan_inputs(
   126→        state,
   127→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   128→        potentials=resolved_options.potentials,
   129→        merge_potentials=resolved_options.merge_potentials,
   130→        codebase_metrics=resolved_options.codebase_metrics,
   131→    )
   132→
   133→    existing = state["findings"]
   134→    ignore_patterns = (
   135→        resolved_options.ignore
   136→        if resolved_options.ignore is not None
   137→        else state.get("config", {}).get("ignore", [])
   138→    )
   139→    current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, current_by_detector, ignored_count, upsert_changed = (
   140→        upsert_findings(
   141→            existing,
   142→            current_findings,
   143→            ignore_patterns,
   144→            now,
   145→            lang=resolved_options.lang,
   146→        )
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    raw_findings = len(current_findings)
   150→    suppressed_pct = _compute_suppression(raw_findings, ignored_count)
   151→
   152→    ran_detectors = (
   153→        set(resolved_options.potentials.keys())
   154→        if resolved_options.potentials is not None
   155→        else None
   156→    )
   157→    suspect_detectors = find_suspect_detectors(
   158→        existing,
   159→        current_by_detector,
   160→        resolved_options.force_resolve,
   161→        ran_detectors,
   162→    )
   163→    auto_resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolve_changed = auto_resolve_disappeared(
   164→        existing,
   165→        current_ids,
   166→        suspect_detectors,
   167→        now,
   168→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   169→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   170→        exclude=resolved_options.exclude,
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    # Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings changed.
   174→    changed_detectors = upsert_changed | resolve_changed
   175→    if changed_detectors:
   176→        _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
   177→            state, changed_detectors=changed_detectors, now=now,
   178→        )
   179→
   180→    _recompute_stats(
   181→        state,
   182→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   183→        subjective_integrity_target=resolved_options.subjective_integrity_target,
   184→    )
   185→    _append_scan_history(
   186→        state,
   187→        now=now,
   188→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   189→        new_count=new_count,
   190→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   191→        ignored_count=ignored_count,
   192→        raw_findings=raw_findings,
   193→        suppressed_pct=suppressed_pct,
   194→        ignore_pattern_count=len(ignore_patterns),
   195→    )
   196→
   197→    chronic_reopeners = [
   198→        finding
   199→        for finding in existing.values()
   200→        if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2 and finding["status"] == "open"
   201→    ]
   202→
   203→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   204→    return _build_merge_diff(
   205→        new_count=new_count,
   206→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   207→        reopened_count=reopened_count,
   208→        current_ids=current_ids,
   209→        suspect_detectors=suspect_detectors,
   210→        chronic_reopeners=chronic_reopeners,
   211→        skipped_other_lang=skipped_other_lang,
   212→        skipped_out_of_scope=skipped_out_of_scope,
   213→        ignored_count=ignored_count,
   214→        ignore_pattern_count=len(ignore_patterns),
   215→        raw_findings=raw_findings,
   216→        suppressed_pct=suppressed_pct,
   217→    )
```

```
1→"""State resolution operations (match + resolve)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "coerce_assessment_score",
     9→    "match_findings",
    10→    "resolve_findings",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import _matches_pattern
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    16→    StateModel,
    17→    ensure_state_defaults,
    18→    utc_now,
    19→    validate_state_invariants,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    22→
    23→
    24→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    25→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    26→
    27→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    28→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    29→    """
    30→    if is_numeric(value):
    31→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    32→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    33→        raw = value.get("score")
    34→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    35→            return None
    36→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    41→    state: StateModel,
    42→    *,
    43→    status: str,
    44→    resolved_findings: list[dict],
    45→    now: str,
    46→) -> None:
    47→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review findings are resolved.
    48→
    49→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    50→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    51→    for a re-review.
    52→    """
    53→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    54→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    58→    for finding in resolved_findings:
    59→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    60→            continue
    61→        dimension = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    64→
    65→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    66→        if dimension not in assessments:
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    70→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    71→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    72→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"
    73→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    74→        else:
    75→            assessments[dimension] = {
    76→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    77→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    78→                "refresh_reason": f"review_finding_{status}",
    79→                "stale_since": now,
    80→            }
    81→
    82→
    83→def match_findings(
    84→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"
    85→) -> list[dict]:
    86→    """Return findings matching *pattern* with the given status."""
    87→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    88→    return [
    89→        finding
    90→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
    91→        if not finding.get("suppressed")
    92→        if (status_filter == "all" or finding["status"] == status_filter)
    93→        and _matches_pattern(finding_id, finding, pattern)
    94→    ]
    95→
    96→
    97→def resolve_findings(
    98→    state: StateModel,
    99→    pattern: str,
   100→    status: str,
   101→    note: str | None = None,
   102→    attestation: str | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Set finding status for matches and return affected finding IDs."""
   105→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   106→    now = utc_now()
   107→    resolved: list[str] = []
   108→    resolved_findings: list[dict] = []
   109→    status_filter = "all" if status == "open" else "open"
   110→    for finding in match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter):
   111→        previous_status = str(finding.get("status", "open")).strip() or "open"
   112→        if status == "open" and previous_status == "open":
   113→            continue
   114→
   115→        extra_updates: dict[str, object] = {}
   116→        if status == "wontfix":
   117→            snapshot_scan_count = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   118→            extra_updates["wontfix_scan_count"] = snapshot_scan_count
   119→            extra_updates["wontfix_snapshot"] = {
   120→                "captured_at": now,
   121→                "scan_count": snapshot_scan_count,
   122→                "tier": finding.get("tier"),
   123→                "confidence": finding.get("confidence"),
   124→                "detail": copy.deepcopy(finding.get("detail", {})),
   125→            }
   126→        if status == "open":
   127→            finding["reopen_count"] = int(finding.get("reopen_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   128→            finding.pop("wontfix_scan_count", None)
   129→            finding.pop("wontfix_snapshot", None)
   130→            previous_note = finding.get("note")
   131→            next_note = note if note is not None else previous_note
   132→            extra_updates["resolved_at"] = None
   133→            extra_updates["note"] = next_note
   134→            extra_updates["resolution_attestation"] = {
   135→                "kind": "manual_reopen",
   136→                "text": attestation or note,
   137→                "attested_at": now,
   138→                "scan_verified": False,
   139→                "previous_status": previous_status,
   140→            }
   141→
   142→        updates: dict[str, object] = {
   143→            "status": status,
   144→            "note": note,
   145→            "resolved_at": now,
   146→            "suppressed": False,
   147→            "suppressed_at": None,
   148→            "suppression_pattern": None,
   149→            "resolution_attestation": {
   150→                "kind": "manual",
   151→                "text": attestation,
   152→                "attested_at": now,
   153→                "scan_verified": False,
   154→            },
   155→        }
   156→        updates.update(extra_updates)
   157→        finding.update(updates)
   158→        resolved.append(finding["id"])
   159→        resolved_findings.append(finding)
   160→
   161→    _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
   162→        state,
   163→        status=status,
   164→        resolved_findings=resolved_findings,
   165→        now=now,
   166→    )
   167→
   168→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   169→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   170→    return resolved
   171→
```

```
    40→_RISK_SEVERITY_ORDER = {
    41→    "critical": 0,
    42→    "high": 1,
    43→    "medium": 2,
    44→    "low": 3,
    45→    "info": 4,
    46→}
    47→
    48→
    49→DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE = 95
    50→MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE = 0
    51→MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE = 100
    52→_HIGH_IGNORE_SUPPRESSION_THRESHOLD = 30.0
    53→_WONTFIX_GAP_THRESHOLD = 1.0
    54→
    55→
    56→def _resolve_target_strict_score(config: dict | None) -> tuple[int, str | None]:
    57→    """Resolve strict-score target from config with bounded fallback."""
    58→    raw_target = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    59→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    60→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE)
    61→    try:
    62→        target = int(raw_target)
    63→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    64→        return (
    65→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    66→            (
    67→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    68→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    69→            ),
    70→        )
    71→    if target < MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE or target > MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE:
    72→        return (
    73→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    74→            (
    75→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    76→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    77→            ),
    78→        )
    79→    return target, None
    80→
    81→
    82→def _compute_strict_target(strict_score: float | None, config: dict | None) -> StrictTarget:
    83→    """Build strict-target context for command rendering and agents."""
    84→    target, warning = _resolve_target_strict_score(config)
    85→    if not isinstance(strict_score, int | float):
    86→        return {
    87→            "target": float(target),
    88→            "current": None,
    89→            "gap": None,
    90→            "state": "unavailable",
    91→            "warning": warning,
    92→        }
    93→
    94→    current = round(float(strict_score), 1)
    95→    gap = round(float(target) - current, 1)
    96→    if gap > 0:
    97→        state = "below"
    98→    elif gap < 0:
    99→        state = "above"
   100→    else:
   101→        state = "at"
   102→    return {
   103→        "target": float(target),
   104→        "current": current,
   105→        "gap": gap,
   106→        "state": state,
   107→        "warning": warning,
   108→    }
   109→
   110→
   111→def _count_open_by_detector(findings: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   112→    """Count open findings by detector, merging structural sub-detectors.
   113→
   114→    When detector is "review" and detail.holistic is True, also increments
   115→    "review_holistic" for separate holistic counting.
   116→    """
   117→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
   118→    for f in findings.values():
   119→        if f["status"] != "open" or f.get("suppressed"):
   120→            continue
   121→        detector = f.get("detector", "unknown")
   122→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
   123→            detector = "structural"
   124→        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
   125→        # Track holistic review findings separately
   126→        if detector == "review" and f.get("detail", {}).get("holistic"):
   127→            by_detector["review_holistic"] = by_detector.get("review_holistic", 0) + 1
   128→    # Track uninvestigated review findings (only when review findings exist)
   129→    if by_detector.get("review", 0) > 0:
   130→        by_detector["review_uninvestigated"] = sum(
   131→            1
   132→            for f in findings.values()
   133→            if f.get("status") == "open"
   134→            and not f.get("suppressed")
   135→            and f.get("detector") == "review"
   136→            and not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   137→        )
   138→    return by_detector
   139→
   140→
   141→def _resolve_badge_path(project_root: Path) -> tuple[str, Path]:
   142→    """Resolve badge path from config, defaulting to root-level scorecard.png."""
   143→    default_rel = "scorecard.png"
   144→    config = {}
   145→    try:
   146→        config = _load_config()
   147→    except (AttributeError, OSError):
   148→        config = {}
   149→
   150→    raw_path = default_rel
   151→    if isinstance(config, dict):
   152→        configured = config.get("badge_path")
   153→        if isinstance(configured, str) and configured.strip():
   154→            raw_path = configured.strip()
   155→
   156→    path = Path(raw_path)
   157→    is_root_anchored = bool(path.root)
   158→    if not path.is_absolute() and not is_root_anchored:
   159→        return raw_path, project_root / path
   160→
   161→    try:
   162→        rel_path = str(path.relative_to(project_root))
   163→    except ValueError:
   164→        rel_path = str(path)
   165→    return rel_path, path
   166→
   167→
   168→def _compute_badge_status() -> BadgeStatus:
   169→    """Check configured scorecard path and whether README references it."""
   170→    project_root = PROJECT_ROOT
   171→
   172→    scorecard_rel, scorecard_path = _resolve_badge_path(project_root)
   173→    generated = scorecard_path.exists()
   174→
   175→    in_readme = False
   176→    if generated:
   177→        for readme_name in ("README.md", "readme.md", "README.MD"):
   178→            readme_path = project_root / readme_name
   179→            if readme_path.exists():
   180→                try:
   181→                    in_readme = scorecard_rel in readme_path.read_text(
   182→                        encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"
   183→                    )
   184→                except OSError:
   185→                    in_readme = False
   186→                break
   187→
   188→    recommendation = None
   189→    if generated and not in_readme:
   190→        recommendation = (
   191→            f'Add to README: <img src="{scorecard_rel}" width="100%">'
   192→        )
   193→
   194→    return {
   195→        "generated": generated,
   196→        "in_readme": in_readme,
   197→        "path": scorecard_rel,
   198→        "recommendation": recommendation,
   199→    }
   200→
   201→
   202→def _compute_primary_action(actions: list[dict]) -> PrimaryAction | None:
   203→    """Pick the highest-priority action for user-facing guidance."""
   204→    if not actions:
   205→        return None
   206→    top = actions[0]
   207→    command = str(top.get("command", "")).strip()
   208→    if not command:
   209→        return None
   210→    description = str(top.get("description", "")).strip() or "run highest-impact action"
   211→    return {
   212→        "command": command,
   213→        "description": description,
   214→    }
   215→
   216→
   217→def _compute_why_now(
   218→    phase: str,
   219→    strategy: dict[str, object],
   220→    primary_action: dict | None,
   221→) -> str:
   222→    """Explain why the recommended action should happen now."""
   223→    hint = str(strategy.get("hint", "")).strip() if isinstance(strategy, dict) else ""
   224→    if hint:
   225→        return hint
   226→    if primary_action and primary_action.get("description"):
   227→        return str(primary_action["description"])
   228→    phase_default = {
   229→        "first_scan": "Start with highest-impact findings to establish a clean baseline.",
   230→        "regression": "Recent regressions should be contained before new work.",
   231→        "stagnation": "Current approach is stalling; tackle a different high-impact lane.",
   232→        "maintenance": "Keep the codebase stable by resolving new risk quickly.",
   233→    }
   234→    return phase_default.get(phase, "Address the highest-impact open findings first.")
   235→
   236→
   237→def _compute_verification_step(_command: str | None) -> VerificationStep:
   238→    """Verification step returned with every narrative plan."""
   239→    return {
   240→        "command": "desloppify scan",
   241→        "reason": "revalidate after changes",
   242→    }
   243→
   244→
   245→def _compute_risk_flags(state: StateModel, debt: dict) -> list[RiskFlag]:
   246→    """Build ordered risk flags from suppression and wontfix debt signals."""
   247→    flags: list[RiskFlag] = []
   248→
   249→    ignore_integrity = state.get("ignore_integrity", {})
   250→    suppressed_pct = float(ignore_integrity.get("suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   251→    ignored_count = int(ignore_integrity.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   252→    if (
   253→        suppressed_pct >= _HIGH_IGNORE_SUPPRESSION_THRESHOLD
   254→        or ignored_count >= 100
   255→    ):
   256→        severity = "high" if suppressed_pct >= 40.0 or ignored_count >= 200 else "medium"
   257→        message = (
   258→            f"{suppressed_pct:.1f}% findings hidden by ignore patterns"
   259→            if suppressed_pct > 0
   260→            else f"{ignored_count} findings hidden by ignore patterns"
   261→        )
   262→        flags.append(
   263→            {
   264→                "type": "high_ignore_suppression",
   265→                "severity": severity,
   266→                "message": message,
   267→            }
   268→        )
   269→
   270→    wontfix_count = int(debt.get("wontfix_count", 0) or 0)
   271→    overall_gap = float(debt.get("overall_gap", 0.0) or 0.0)
   272→    if overall_gap >= _WONTFIX_GAP_THRESHOLD or wontfix_count > 0:
   273→        severity = "high" if overall_gap >= 5.0 or wontfix_count >= 50 else "medium"
   274→        flags.append(
   275→            {
   276→                "type": "wontfix_gap",
   277→                "severity": severity,
   278→                "message": (
   279→                    f"Strict/lenient gap is {overall_gap:.1f} pts with "
   280→                    f"{wontfix_count} wontfix findings"
   281→                ),
   282→            }
   283→        )
   284→
   285→    flags.sort(key=lambda flag: _RISK_SEVERITY_ORDER.get(flag.get("severity"), 99))
   286→    return flags
   287→
   288→
   289→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   290→class NarrativeContext:
   291→    """Optional context inputs for narrative computation."""
   292→
   293→    diff: dict | None = None
   294→    lang: str | None = None
   295→    command: str | None = None
   296→    config: dict | None = None
   297→
   298→def _history_for_lang(raw_history: list[dict], lang: str | None) -> list[dict]:
   299→    if not lang:
   300→        return raw_history
   301→    return [entry for entry in raw_history if entry.get("lang") in (lang, None)]
   302→
   303→
   304→def _scoped_findings(state: StateModel) -> dict[str, Finding]:
   305→    return path_scoped_findings(
   306→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   307→    )
   308→
   309→
   310→def _score_snapshot(state: StateModel) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
   311→    return get_strict_score(state), get_overall_score(state)
   312→
   313→
   314→def compute_narrative(
   315→    state: StateModel,
   316→    context: NarrativeContext | None = None,
   317→) -> NarrativeResult:
   318→    """Compute structured narrative context from state data."""
   319→    resolved_context = context or NarrativeContext()
   320→
   321→    diff = resolved_context.diff
   322→    lang = resolved_context.lang
   323→    command = resolved_context.command
   324→    config = resolved_context.config
   325→
   326→    raw_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   327→    history = _history_for_lang(raw_history, lang)
   328→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   329→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   330→    strict_score, overall_score = _score_snapshot(state)
   331→    findings = _scoped_findings(state)
   332→
   333→    by_detector = _count_open_by_detector(findings)
   334→    badge = _compute_badge_status()
   335→
   336→    phase = _detect_phase(history, strict_score)
   337→    dimensions = _analyze_dimensions(dim_scores, history, state)
   338→    debt = _analyze_debt(dim_scores, findings, history)
   339→    milestone = _detect_milestone(state, None, history)
   340→    action_context = ActionContext(
   341→        by_detector=by_detector,
   342→        dimension_scores=dim_scores,
   343→        state=state,
   344→        debt=debt,
   345→        lang=lang,
   346→    )
   347→    actions = [dict(action) for action in compute_actions(action_context)]
   348→    strategy = compute_strategy(findings, by_detector, actions, phase, lang)
   349→    tools = dict(compute_tools(by_detector, state, lang, badge))
   350→    primary_action = _compute_primary_action(actions)
   351→    why_now = _compute_why_now(phase, strategy, primary_action)
   352→    verification_step = _compute_verification_step(command)
   353→    risk_flags = _compute_risk_flags(state, debt)
   354→    strict_target = _compute_strict_target(strict_score, config)
   355→    headline = _compute_headline(
   356→        phase,
   357→        dimensions,
   358→        debt,
   359→        milestone,
   360→        diff,
   361→        strict_score,
   362→        overall_score,
   363→        stats,
   364→        history,
   365→        open_by_detector=by_detector,
   366→    )
   367→    reminders, updated_reminder_history = _compute_reminders(
   368→        state, lang, phase, debt, actions, dimensions, badge, command, config=config
   369→    )
   370→
   371→    return {
   372→        "phase": phase,
   373→        "headline": headline,
   374→        "dimensions": dimensions,
   375→        "actions": actions,
   376→        "strategy": strategy,
   377→        "tools": tools,
   378→        "debt": debt,
   379→        "milestone": milestone,
   380→        "primary_action": primary_action,
   381→        "why_now": why_now,
   382→        "verification_step": verification_step,
   383→        "risk_flags": risk_flags,
   384→        "strict_target": strict_target,
   385→        "reminders": reminders,
   386→        "reminder_history": updated_reminder_history,
   387→    }
   388→
```

```
1→"""Execution strategy engine for narrative lanes and parallelization hints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Iterable
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import (
     9→    _DETECTOR_CASCADE,
    10→    STRUCTURAL_MERGE,
    11→)
    12→
    13→
    14→def open_files_by_detector(findings: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, set[str]]:
    15→    """Collect file sets of open findings by detector."""
    16→    by_detector: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    17→    for finding in findings.values():
    18→        if finding["status"] != "open" or finding.get("suppressed"):
    19→            continue
    20→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
    21→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
    22→            detector = "structural"
    23→        file_path = finding.get("file", "")
    24→        if not file_path:
    25→            by_detector.setdefault(detector, set())
    26→            continue
    27→        by_detector.setdefault(detector, set()).add(file_path)
    28→    return by_detector
    29→
    30→
    31→def compute_fixer_leverage(
    32→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
    33→    actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
    34→    phase: str,
    35→    _lang: str | None,
    36→) -> dict[str, float | int | str]:
    37→    """Estimate how much value automated fixers would deliver."""
    38→    auto_fixable = sum(
    39→        action.get("count", 0) for action in actions if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"
    40→    )
    41→    total = sum(by_detector.values())
    42→    coverage = auto_fixable / total if total > 0 else 0.0
    43→    total_impact = sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action in actions)
    44→    auto_impact = sum(
    45→        action.get("impact", 0)
    46→        for action in actions
    47→        if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"
    48→    )
    49→    impact_ratio = auto_impact / total_impact if total_impact > 0 else 0.0
    50→
    51→    if coverage == 0:
    52→        recommendation = "none"
    53→    elif coverage > 0.4 or impact_ratio > 0.3:
    54→        recommendation = "strong"
    55→    elif phase in ("first_scan", "stagnation", "regression") and coverage > 0.15:
    56→        recommendation = "strong"
    57→    elif coverage > 0.1:
    58→        recommendation = "moderate"
    59→    else:
    60→        recommendation = "none"
    61→
    62→    return {
    63→        "auto_fixable_count": auto_fixable,
    64→        "total_count": total,
    65→        "coverage": round(coverage, 3),
    66→        "impact_ratio": round(impact_ratio, 3),
    67→        "recommendation": recommendation,
    68→    }
    69→
    70→
    71→def _cleanup_lane_actions(actions: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    72→    cascade_rank = {detector: idx for idx, detector in enumerate(_DETECTOR_CASCADE)}
    73→
    74→    def sort_key(action: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[int, float]:
    75→        detector = action.get("detector", "")
    76→        return (cascade_rank.get(detector, 99), -action.get("impact", 0))
    77→
    78→    return sorted(actions, key=sort_key)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _files_for_actions(
    82→    actions: Iterable[dict[str, Any]], files_by_detector: dict[str, set[str]]
    83→) -> set[str]:
    84→    files: set[str] = set()
    85→    for action in actions:
    86→        detector = action.get("detector")
    87→        if detector and detector in files_by_detector:
    88→            files |= files_by_detector[detector]
    89→    return files
    90→
    91→
    92→def _group_by_file_overlap(
    93→    action_files: list[tuple[dict[str, Any], set[str]]],
    94→) -> list[list[tuple[dict[str, Any], set[str]]]]:
    95→    """Group actions whose file sets overlap using union-find."""
    96→    item_count = len(action_files)
    97→    if item_count == 0:
    98→        return []
    99→
   100→    parent = list(range(item_count))
   101→
   102→    def find(index: int) -> int:
   103→        while parent[index] != index:
   104→            parent[index] = parent[parent[index]]
   105→            index = parent[index]
   106→        return index
   107→
   108→    def union(left: int, right: int) -> None:
   109→        left_root, right_root = find(left), find(right)
   110→        if left_root != right_root:
   111→            parent[left_root] = right_root
   112→
   113→    for left in range(item_count):
   114→        for right in range(left + 1, item_count):
   115→            if action_files[left][1] & action_files[right][1]:
   116→                union(left, right)
   117→
   118→    grouped_indices: dict[int, list[int]] = {}
   119→    for index in range(item_count):
   120→        grouped_indices.setdefault(find(index), []).append(index)
   121→
   122→    return [
   123→        [action_files[index] for index in indices]
   124→        for indices in grouped_indices.values()
   125→    ]
   126→
   127→
   128→def _refactor_lanes(
   129→    refactor_actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
   130→    files_by_detector: dict[str, set[str]],
   131→) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   132→    """Split refactor work into independent lanes by file overlap."""
   133→    lanes: dict[str, dict] = {}
   134→    action_files = [
   135→        (action, files_by_detector.get(action.get("detector"), set()))
   136→        for action in refactor_actions
   137→    ]
   138→
   139→    test_coverage_actions = [
   140→        (action, files)
   141→        for action, files in action_files
   142→        if action.get("detector") == "test_coverage"
   143→    ]
   144→    other_actions = [
   145→        (action, files)
   146→        for action, files in action_files
   147→        if action.get("detector") != "test_coverage"
   148→    ]
   149→
   150→    groups = _group_by_file_overlap(other_actions)
   151→    for index, group in enumerate(groups):
   152→        lane_name = f"refactor_{index}" if len(groups) > 1 else "refactor"
   153→        lanes[lane_name] = {
   154→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action, _ in group],
   155→            "file_count": len(
   156→                _files_for_actions((action for action, _ in group), files_by_detector)
   157→            ),
   158→            "total_impact": round(
   159→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action, _ in group), 1
   160→            ),
   161→            "automation": "manual",
   162→            "run_first": False,
   163→        }
   164→
   165→    if test_coverage_actions:
   166→        lanes["test_coverage"] = {
   167→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action, _ in test_coverage_actions],
   168→            "file_count": len(
   169→                _files_for_actions(
   170→                    (action for action, _ in test_coverage_actions), files_by_detector
   171→                )
   172→            ),
   173→            "total_impact": round(
   174→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action, _ in test_coverage_actions), 1
   175→            ),
   176→            "automation": "manual",
   177→            "run_first": False,
   178→        }
   179→
   180→    return lanes
   181→
   182→
   183→def compute_lanes(
   184→    actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
   185→    files_by_detector: dict[str, set[str]],
   186→) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   187→    """Partition actions into parallelizable work lanes."""
   188→    lanes: dict[str, dict] = {}
   189→
   190→    cleanup_actions = _cleanup_lane_actions(
   191→        [action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"]
   192→    )
   193→    reorganize_actions = [
   194→        action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "reorganize"
   195→    ]
   196→    debt_actions = [action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "debt_review"]
   197→    refactor_actions = [
   198→        action
   199→        for action in actions
   200→        if action.get("type") not in {"auto_fix", "reorganize", "debt_review"}
   201→    ]
   202→
   203→    if cleanup_actions:
   204→        cleanup_files = _files_for_actions(cleanup_actions, files_by_detector)
   205→        lanes["cleanup"] = {
   206→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action in cleanup_actions],
   207→            "file_count": len(cleanup_files),
   208→            "total_impact": round(
   209→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action in cleanup_actions), 1
   210→            ),
   211→            "automation": "full",
   212→            "run_first": False,
   213→        }
   214→
   215→    if reorganize_actions:
   216→        lanes["restructure"] = {
   217→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action in reorganize_actions],
   218→            "file_count": len(
   219→                _files_for_actions(reorganize_actions, files_by_detector)
   220→            ),
   221→            "total_impact": round(
   222→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action in reorganize_actions), 1
   223→            ),
   224→            "automation": "manual",
   225→            "run_first": False,
   226→        }
   227→
   228→    if refactor_actions:
   229→        lanes.update(_refactor_lanes(refactor_actions, files_by_detector))
   230→
   231→    if debt_actions:
   232→        lanes["debt_review"] = {
   233→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action in debt_actions],
   234→            "file_count": 0,
   235→            "total_impact": 0.0,
   236→            "automation": "manual",
   237→            "run_first": False,
   238→        }
   239→
   240→    if "cleanup" in lanes:
   241→        for lane_name, lane in lanes.items():
   242→            if lane_name == "cleanup":
   243→                continue
   244→            lane_files = _files_for_actions(
   245→                (action for action in actions if action["priority"] in lane["actions"]),
   246→                files_by_detector,
   247→            )
   248→            if cleanup_files & lane_files:
   249→                lanes["cleanup"]["run_first"] = True
   250→                break
   251→
   252→    return lanes
   253→
   254→
   255→def _significant_lane(lane_name: str, lane: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   256→    if lane_name == "debt_review" or lane.get("run_first"):
   257→        return False
   258→    return lane.get("file_count", 0) >= 5 or lane.get("total_impact", 0) >= 1.0
   259→
   260→
   261→def compute_strategy_hint(
   262→    fixer_leverage: dict[str, Any],
   263→    lanes: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
   264→    can_parallelize: bool,
   265→    phase: str,
   266→) -> str:
   267→    """Generate one- or two-sentence execution strategy guidance."""
   268→    recommendation = fixer_leverage.get("recommendation", "none")
   269→    coverage_pct = round(fixer_leverage.get("coverage", 0) * 100)
   270→    lane_count = sum(
   271→        1
   272→        for lane_name, lane in lanes.items()
   273→        if lane_name != "debt_review" and not lane.get("run_first")
   274→    )
   275→
   276→    if recommendation == "strong" and can_parallelize:
   277→        return (
   278→            f"Run fixers first — they cover {coverage_pct}% of findings. "
   279→            f"Then {lane_count} independent workstreams, safe to parallelize. "
   280→            "Rescan after each phase to verify."
   281→        )
   282→    if recommendation == "strong":
   283→        return (
   284→            f"Run fixers first — they cover {coverage_pct}% of findings. "
   285→            "Then rescan to verify."
   286→        )
   287→    if can_parallelize:
   288→        return (
   289→            f"{lane_count} independent workstreams, safe to parallelize. "
   290→            "Rescan after each phase to verify."
   291→        )
   292→    if phase == "maintenance":
   293→        return "Maintenance mode — address new findings as they appear."
   294→    if phase == "stagnation":
   295→        return "Try a different dimension to break the plateau."
   296→    return "Work through actions in priority order. Rescan after each fix to track progress."
   297→
   298→
   299→def compute_strategy(
   300→    findings: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
   301→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
   302→    actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
   303→    phase: str,
   304→    lang: str | None,
   305→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   306→    """Orchestrate strategy computation and annotate actions with lanes."""
   307→    files_by_detector = open_files_by_detector(findings)
   308→    fixer_leverage = compute_fixer_leverage(by_detector, actions, phase, lang)
   309→    lanes = compute_lanes(actions, files_by_detector)
   310→
   311→    action_lane: dict[int, str] = {}
   312→    for lane_name, lane in lanes.items():
   313→        for priority in lane["actions"]:
   314→            action_lane[priority] = lane_name
   315→    for action in actions:
   316→        action["lane"] = action_lane.get(action["priority"])
   317→
   318→    significant_non_blocked = [
   319→        (lane_name, lane)
   320→        for lane_name, lane in lanes.items()
   321→        if _significant_lane(lane_name, lane)
   322→    ]
   323→    can_parallelize = len(significant_non_blocked) >= 2
   324→
   325→    hint = compute_strategy_hint(fixer_leverage, lanes, can_parallelize, phase)
   326→    review_action = next(
   327→        (action for action in actions if action.get("detector") == "review"), None
   328→    )
   329→    if review_action:
   330→        hint += f" Review: {review_action['count']} finding(s) — `desloppify show review --status open`."
   331→
   332→    return {
   333→        "fixer_leverage": fixer_leverage,
   334→        "lanes": {name: {**lane} for name, lane in lanes.items()},
   335→        "can_parallelize": can_parallelize,
   336→        "hint": hint,
   337→    }
   338→
```

```
1→"""Contextual reminders with decay."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→import logging
     6→from datetime import UTC
     7→from datetime import datetime as _dt
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    10→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import (
    11→    _FEEDBACK_URL,
    12→    _REMINDER_DECAY_THRESHOLD,
    13→    STRUCTURAL_MERGE,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.state import StateModel, get_strict_score, path_scoped_findings
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→
    20→def _compute_fp_rates(findings: dict) -> dict[tuple[str, str], float]:
    21→    """Compute false_positive rate per (detector, zone) from historical findings."""
    22→    counts: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, int]] = {}
    23→    for finding in findings.values():
    24→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
    25→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
    26→            detector = "structural"
    27→        zone = finding.get("zone", "production")
    28→        key = (detector, zone)
    29→        if key not in counts:
    30→            counts[key] = {"total": 0, "fp": 0}
    31→        counts[key]["total"] += 1
    32→        if finding.get("status") == "false_positive":
    33→            counts[key]["fp"] += 1
    34→
    35→    rates: dict[tuple[str, str], float] = {}
    36→    for key, stat in counts.items():
    37→        if stat["total"] >= 5 and stat["fp"] > 0:
    38→            rates[key] = stat["fp"] / stat["total"]
    39→    return rates
    40→
    41→
    42→def _auto_fixer_reminder(actions: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    43→    auto_fix_actions = [
    44→        action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"
    45→    ]
    46→    if not auto_fix_actions:
    47→        return []
    48→    total = sum(action.get("count", 0) for action in auto_fix_actions)
    49→    if total <= 0:
    50→        return []
    51→    first_cmd = auto_fix_actions[0].get("command", "desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run")
    52→    return [
    53→        {
    54→            "type": "auto_fixers_available",
    55→            "message": f"{total} findings are auto-fixable. Run `{first_cmd}`.",
    56→            "command": first_cmd,
    57→        }
    58→    ]
    59→
    60→
    61→def _rescan_needed_reminder(command: str | None) -> list[dict]:
    62→    if command not in {"fix", "resolve", "ignore"}:
    63→        return []
    64→    return [
    65→        {
    66→            "type": "rescan_needed",
    67→            "message": "Rescan to verify — cascading effects may create new findings.",
    68→            "command": "desloppify scan",
    69→        }
    70→    ]
    71→
    72→
    73→def _badge_reminder(strict_score: float | None, badge: dict) -> list[dict]:
    74→    eligible_for_badge = (
    75→        strict_score is not None
    76→        and strict_score >= 90
    77→        and badge.get("generated")
    78→        and not badge.get("in_readme")
    79→    )
    80→    if not eligible_for_badge:
    81→        return []
    82→    badge_path = str(badge.get("path") or "scorecard.png")
    83→    return [
    84→        {
    85→            "type": "badge_recommendation",
    86→            "message": (
    87→                "Score is above 90! Add the scorecard to your README: "
    88→                f'<img src="{badge_path}" width="100%">'
    89→            ),
    90→            "command": None,
    91→        }
    92→    ]
    93→
    94→
    95→def _wontfix_debt_reminders(state: StateModel, debt: dict, command: str | None) -> list[dict]:
    96→    reminders: list[dict] = []
    97→    if debt.get("trend") == "growing":
    98→        reminders.append(
    99→            {
   100→                "type": "wontfix_growing",
   101→                "message": "Wontfix debt is growing. Review stale decisions: `desloppify show --status wontfix`.",
   102→                "command": "desloppify show --status wontfix",
   103→            }
   104→        )
   105→
   106→    scan_count = len(state.get("scan_history", []))
   107→    if scan_count < 20 or command != "scan":
   108→        return reminders
   109→
   110→    stale_wontfix = []
   111→    for finding in state.get("findings", {}).values():
   112→        if finding.get("status") != "wontfix":
   113→            continue
   114→        resolved_at = finding.get("resolved_at")
   115→        if not resolved_at:
   116→            continue
   117→        try:
   118→            age_days = (_dt.now(UTC) - _dt.fromisoformat(resolved_at)).days
   119→        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
   120→            log_best_effort_failure(
   121→                logger, f"parse wontfix timestamp {resolved_at!r}", exc
   122→            )
   123→            continue
   124→        if age_days > 60:
   125→            stale_wontfix.append(finding)
   126→
   127→    if stale_wontfix:
   128→        reminders.append(
   129→            {
   130→                "type": "wontfix_stale",
   131→                "message": (
   132→                    f"{len(stale_wontfix)} wontfix item(s) are >60 days old. "
   133→                    f"Has anything changed? Review with: "
   134→                    f'`desloppify show "*" --status wontfix`'
   135→                ),
   136→                "command": 'desloppify show "*" --status wontfix',
   137→            }
   138→        )
   139→    return reminders
   140→
   141→
   142→def _ignore_suppression_reminder(state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
   143→    """Nudge when ignores/suppression are high enough to mask signal quality."""
   144→    integrity = state.get("ignore_integrity", {}) or {}
   145→    ignored = int(integrity.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   146→    suppressed_pct = float(integrity.get("suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   147→    if ignored < 10 and suppressed_pct < 30.0:
   148→        return []
   149→    return [
   150→        {
   151→            "type": "ignore_suppression_high",
   152→            "message": (
   153→                f"Ignore suppression is high ({ignored} ignored, {suppressed_pct:.1f}% suppressed). "
   154→                "Revisit broad ignore patterns and resolve stale suppressions."
   155→            ),
   156→            "command": "desloppify show --status wontfix",
   157→        }
   158→    ]
   159→
   160→
   161→def _stagnation_reminders(dimensions: dict) -> list[dict]:
   162→    reminders: list[dict] = []
   163→    for dim in dimensions.get("stagnant_dimensions", []):
   164→        strict = dim.get("strict", 0)
   165→        if strict >= 99:
   166→            message = (
   167→                f"{dim['name']} has been at {strict}% for {dim['stuck_scans']} scans. "
   168→                f"The remaining items may be worth marking as wontfix if they're intentional."
   169→            )
   170→        else:
   171→            message = (
   172→                f"{dim['name']} has been stuck at {strict}% for {dim['stuck_scans']} scans. "
   173→                f"Try tackling it from a different angle — run `desloppify next` to find the right entry point."
   174→            )
   175→        reminders.append(
   176→            {
   177→                "type": "stagnant_nudge",
   178→                "message": message,
   179→                "command": None,
   180→            }
   181→        )
   182→    return reminders
   183→
   184→
   185→def _dry_run_reminder(actions: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
   186→    if not actions or actions[0].get("type") != "auto_fix":
   187→        return []
   188→    return [
   189→        {
   190→            "type": "dry_run_first",
   191→            "message": "Always --dry-run first, review changes, then apply.",
   192→            "command": None,
   193→        }
   194→    ]
   195→
   196→
   197→def _zone_classification_reminder(state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
   198→    zone_dist = state.get("zone_distribution")
   199→    if not zone_dist:
   200→        return []
   201→    non_prod = sum(value for key, value in zone_dist.items() if key != "production")
   202→    if non_prod <= 0:
   203→        return []
   204→    total = sum(zone_dist.values())
   205→    parts = [
   206→        f"{value} {key}"
   207→        for key, value in sorted(zone_dist.items())
   208→        if key != "production" and value > 0
   209→    ]
   210→    return [
   211→        {
   212→            "type": "zone_classification",
   213→            "message": (
   214→                f"{non_prod} of {total} files classified as non-production "
   215→                f"({', '.join(parts)}). "
   216→                f"Override with `desloppify zone set <file> production` "
   217→                f"if any are misclassified."
   218→            ),
   219→            "command": "desloppify zone show",
   220→        }
   221→    ]
   222→
   223→
   224→def _fp_calibration_reminders(fp_rates: dict[tuple[str, str], float]) -> list[dict]:
   225→    reminders: list[dict] = []
   226→    for (detector, zone), rate in fp_rates.items():
   227→        if rate <= 0.3:
   228→            continue
   229→        pct = round(rate * 100)
   230→        reminders.append(
   231→            {
   232→                "type": f"fp_calibration_{detector}_{zone}",
   233→                "message": (
   234→                    f"{pct}% of {detector} findings in {zone} zone are false positives. "
   235→                    f"Consider reviewing detection rules for {zone} files."
   236→                ),
   237→                "command": None,
   238→            }
   239→        )
   240→    return reminders
   241→
   242→
   243→def _review_queue_reminders(
   244→    state: StateModel,
   245→    scoped_findings: dict,
   246→    command: str | None,
   247→    strict_score: float | None,
   248→) -> list[dict]:
   249→    reminders: list[dict] = []
   250→    open_review = [
   251→        finding
   252→        for finding in scoped_findings.values()
   253→        if finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("detector") == "review"
   254→    ]
   255→    if open_review:
   256→        uninvestigated = [
   257→            finding
   258→            for finding in open_review
   259→            if not finding.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   260→        ]
   261→        if uninvestigated:
   262→            reminders.append(
   263→                {
   264→                    "type": "review_findings_pending",
   265→                    "message": f"{len(uninvestigated)} review finding(s) need investigation. "
   266→                    f"Run `desloppify show review --status open` to see the work queue.",
   267→                    "command": "desloppify show review --status open",
   268→                }
   269→            )
   270→
   271→    if command == "resolve" and state.get("subjective_assessments"):
   272→        reminders.append(
   273→            {
   274→                "type": "rereview_needed",
   275→                "message": "Subjective results may be stale after resolve. Re-run "
   276→                "`desloppify review --prepare` to refresh, or reset with "
   277→                "`desloppify scan --reset-subjective` before a clean rerun.",
   278→                "command": "desloppify review --prepare",
   279→            }
   280→        )
   281→
   282→    review_cache = state.get("review_cache", {})
   283→    if not review_cache.get("files"):
   284→        current_strict = strict_score or 0
   285→        if current_strict >= 80:
   286→            reminders.append(
   287→                {
   288→                    "type": "review_not_run",
   289→                    "message": (
   290→                        "Mechanical checks look good! Run a subjective design review "
   291→                        "to catch issues linters miss: desloppify review --prepare"
   292→                    ),
   293→                    "command": "desloppify review --prepare",
   294→                }
   295→            )
   296→
   297→    return reminders
   298→
   299→
   300→def _has_open_findings(state: StateModel) -> bool:
   301→    """True when any non-suppressed open findings remain in the queue."""
   302→    return any(
   303→        f.get("status") == "open" and not f.get("suppressed")
   304→        for f in (state.get("findings") or {}).values()
   305→    )
   306→
   307→
   308→def _stale_assessment_reminder(state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
   309→    """Nudge when mechanical changes have staled subjective assessments."""
   310→    if _has_open_findings(state):
   311→        return []
   312→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
   313→    stale_dims = [
   314→        dim_key
   315→        for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items()
   316→        if isinstance(assessment, dict) and assessment.get("needs_review_refresh")
   317→    ]
   318→    if not stale_dims:
   319→        return []
   320→    dims_arg = ",".join(stale_dims[:10])
   321→    return [
   322→        {
   323→            "type": "stale_assessments",
   324→            "message": (
   325→                f"{len(stale_dims)} subjective dimension{'s' if len(stale_dims) != 1 else ''} "
   326→                f"stale after mechanical changes — re-review with: "
   327→                f"`desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}`"
   328→            ),
   329→            "command": f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}",
   330→        }
   331→    ]
   332→
   333→
   334→def _review_staleness_reminder(state: StateModel, config: dict | None) -> list[dict]:
   335→    review_max_age = (config or {}).get("review_max_age_days", 30)
   336→    review_cache = state.get("review_cache", {})
   337→    if review_max_age <= 0 or not review_cache.get("files"):
   338→        return []
   339→    try:
   340→        oldest_str = min(
   341→            review["reviewed_at"]
   342→            for review in review_cache["files"].values()
   343→            if review.get("reviewed_at")
   344→        )
   345→        oldest = _dt.fromisoformat(oldest_str)
   346→        age_days = (_dt.now(UTC) - oldest).days
   347→    except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
   348→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "parse oldest review timestamp", exc)
   349→        return []
   350→    if age_days <= review_max_age:
   351→        return []
   352→    return [
   353→        {
   354→            "type": "review_stale",
   355→            "message": f"Design review is {age_days} days old — run: desloppify review --prepare",
   356→            "command": "desloppify review --prepare",
   357→        }
   358→    ]
   359→
   360→
   361→def _feedback_reminder(
   362→    state: StateModel,
   363→    phase: str,
   364→    command: str | None,
   365→    fp_rates: dict[tuple[str, str], float],
   366→) -> list[dict]:
   367→    scan_count = len(state.get("scan_history", []))
   368→    if scan_count < 2 or command != "scan":
   369→        return []
   370→    high_fp_dets = [
   371→        detector for (detector, _zone), rate in fp_rates.items() if rate > 0.3
   372→    ]
   373→    if high_fp_dets:
   374→        nudge_msg = (
   375→            f"Some detectors have high false-positive rates ({', '.join(high_fp_dets)}). "
   376→            f"If patterns are being misclassified, file an issue at "
   377→            f"{_FEEDBACK_URL} with the file and expected behavior — "
   378→            f"it helps calibrate detection for everyone."
   379→        )
   380→    elif phase == "stagnation":
   381→        nudge_msg = (
   382→            f"Score has plateaued — if you suspect desloppify is missing patterns "
   383→            f"or not capturing something it should, file an issue at "
   384→            f"{_FEEDBACK_URL} describing what you expected. "
   385→            f"Gaps in detection are a common cause of stagnation."
   386→        )
   387→    else:
   388→        nudge_msg = (
   389→            f"If you notice patterns desloppify doesn't detect, false positives, "
   390→            f"or findings that seem off, file an issue at {_FEEDBACK_URL} — "
   391→            f"include the file and what you expected. It helps improve the tool."
   392→        )
   393→    return [
   394→        {
   395→            "type": "feedback_nudge",
   396→            "message": nudge_msg,
   397→            "command": None,
   398→        }
   399→    ]
   400→
   401→
   402→_REMINDER_METADATA: dict[str, tuple[int, str]] = {
   403→    # Highest urgency operational nudges.
   404→    "rescan_needed": (1, "high"),
   405→    "ignore_suppression_high": (1, "high"),
   406→    "review_findings_pending": (1, "high"),
   407→    "rereview_needed": (1, "high"),
   408→    # Medium urgency quality/process nudges.
   409→    "wontfix_growing": (2, "medium"),
   410→    "wontfix_stale": (2, "medium"),
   411→    "stagnant_nudge": (2, "medium"),
   412→    "review_stale": (2, "medium"),
   413→    "stale_assessments": (2, "medium"),
   414→    "auto_fixers_available": (2, "medium"),
   415→    "zone_classification": (2, "medium"),
   416→    "fp_calibration": (2, "medium"),
   417→    # Informational nudges.
   418→    "badge_recommendation": (3, "low"),
   419→    "dry_run_first": (3, "low"),
   420→    "feedback_nudge": (3, "low"),
   421→    "review_not_run": (3, "low"),
   422→    "report_scores": (3, "low"),
   423→}
   424→
   425→
   426→def _decorate_reminder_metadata(reminders: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
   427→    """Attach stable priority/severity metadata and sort by priority."""
   428→    decorated: list[dict] = []
   429→    for reminder in reminders:
   430→        reminder_type = str(reminder.get("type", ""))
   431→        key = reminder_type
   432→        if reminder_type.startswith("fp_calibration_"):
   433→            key = "fp_calibration"
   434→        priority, severity = _REMINDER_METADATA.get(key, (3, "low"))
   435→        clone = dict(reminder)
   436→        clone.setdefault("priority", priority)
   437→        clone.setdefault("severity", severity)
   438→        decorated.append(clone)
   439→    decorated.sort(key=lambda item: (int(item.get("priority", 3)), str(item.get("type", ""))))
   440→    return decorated
   441→
   442→
   443→def _report_scores_reminder(command: str | None) -> list[dict]:
   444→    if command != "scan":
   445→        return []
   446→    return [
   447→        {
   448→            "type": "report_scores",
   449→            "message": (
   450→                "ALWAYS share ALL scores with the user: overall, objective, and strict, "
   451→                "plus every dimension score (lenient + strict), including subjective dimensions. "
   452→                "The goal is to maximize strict scores."
   453→            ),
   454→            "command": None,
   455→            "no_decay": True,
   456→        }
   457→    ]
   458→
   459→
   460→def _apply_decay(
   461→    reminders: list[dict], reminder_history: dict
   462→) -> tuple[list[dict], dict]:
   463→    filtered: list[dict] = []
   464→    for reminder in reminders:
   465→        if reminder.get("no_decay"):
   466→            filtered.append(reminder)
   467→            continue
   468→        count = reminder_history.get(reminder["type"], 0)
   469→        if count < _REMINDER_DECAY_THRESHOLD:
   470→            filtered.append(reminder)
   471→
   472→    updated_history = dict(reminder_history)
   473→    for reminder in filtered:
   474→        updated_history[reminder["type"]] = updated_history.get(reminder["type"], 0) + 1
   475→    return filtered, updated_history
   476→
   477→
   478→def _compute_reminders(
   479→    state: StateModel,
   480→    lang: str | None,
   481→    phase: str,
   482→    debt: dict,
   483→    actions: list[dict],
   484→    dimensions: dict,
   485→    badge: dict,
   486→    command: str | None,
   487→    config: dict | None = None,
   488→) -> tuple[list[dict], dict]:
   489→    """Compute context-specific reminders, suppressing those shown too many times."""
   490→    del lang  # Reserved for future language-specific rules.
   491→
   492→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state)
   493→    reminder_history = state.get("reminder_history", {})
   494→    scoped_findings = path_scoped_findings(
   495→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   496→    )
   497→    fp_rates = _compute_fp_rates(scoped_findings)
   498→
   499→    reminders: list[dict] = []
   500→    reminders.extend(_report_scores_reminder(command))
   501→    reminders.extend(_auto_fixer_reminder(actions))
   502→    reminders.extend(_rescan_needed_reminder(command))
   503→    reminders.extend(_badge_reminder(strict_score, badge))
   504→    reminders.extend(_wontfix_debt_reminders(state, debt, command))
   505→    reminders.extend(_ignore_suppression_reminder(state))
   506→    reminders.extend(_stagnation_reminders(dimensions))
   507→    reminders.extend(_dry_run_reminder(actions))
   508→    reminders.extend(_zone_classification_reminder(state))
   509→    reminders.extend(_fp_calibration_reminders(fp_rates))
   510→    reminders.extend(
   511→        _review_queue_reminders(state, scoped_findings, command, strict_score)
   512→    )
   513→    reminders.extend(_stale_assessment_reminder(state))
   514→    reminders.extend(_review_staleness_reminder(state, config))
   515→    reminders.extend(_feedback_reminder(state, phase, command, fp_rates))
   516→
   517→    reminders = _decorate_reminder_metadata(reminders)
   518→    return _apply_decay(reminders, reminder_history)
   519→
```

```
     9→
    10→def _finding(
    11→    *,
    12→    id: str,
    13→    detector: str,
    14→    file: str,
    15→    tier: int = 2,
    16→    detail: dict | None = None,
    17→    status: str = "open",
    18→) -> dict:
    19→    return {
    20→        "id": id,
    21→        "detector": detector,
    22→        "file": file,
    23→        "tier": tier,
    24→        "confidence": "high",
    25→        "summary": f"{detector} finding in {file}",
    26→        "detail": detail or {},
    27→        "status": status,
    28→        "note": None,
    29→        "first_seen": "2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
    30→        "last_seen": "2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
    31→        "resolved_at": None,
    32→        "reopen_count": 0,
    33→    }
```

Now let me compile the findings into a comprehensive report:

## Summary: Subjective Dimension Review Gating Logic

I've conducted a thorough search of the desloppify codebase and found a well-designed system for blocking/gating subjective dimension reviews when objective findings exist in the queue. Here are the key components:

### 1. **Main Gate Implementation** (`desloppify/app/commands/review/preflight.py`)

**Entry Point:** `review_rerun_preflight()` function

The gate logic prevents re-review of subjective dimensions when:
- The dimension **already has a nonzero score** (indicating a prior review)
- **Open objective findings still exist** in the work queue
- The user has NOT provided `--force-review-rerun` flag

**How it works:**
- Checks if targeted dimensions are "scored" (have nonzero subjective scores)
- Only gates dimensions that are actually targeted (via `--dimensions` flag or all scored dims)
- First review runs (all zero scores) bypass the gate entirely
- Builds the work queue with `QueueBuildOptions(status="open", include_subjective=False, count=None)`
- Counts total objective findings and blocks if > 0 with exit code 1

**Lines 59-129 in preflight.py:**
```python
def review_rerun_preflight(state, args, *, state_file=None, save_fn=save_state) -> None:
    """Single entry point: gate check -> clear stale -> save."""
    
    # Get targeted dimensions
    dimensions = parse_dimensions(args)
    
    # --force-review-rerun bypasses entirely
    if getattr(args, "force_review_rerun", False):
        print(colorize("--force-review-rerun: bypassing objective-plan-drained check.", "yellow"))
    else:
        scored_dims = _scored_dimensions(state)  # Find dims with nonzero scores
        blocking_dims = sorted(d for d in scored_dims if d in dimensions)
        
        if blocking_dims:
            result = build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(
                status="open", include_subjective=False, count=None))
            total = result["total"]
            
            if total > 0:  # Block if open objectives exist
                print(f"Blocked: {total} open objective finding(s)", file=sys.stderr)
                print(f"Scored dimensions: {', '.join(blocking_dims)}", file=sys.stderr)
                sys.exit(1)
    
    # Gate passed: clear stale markers and save
    cleared = clear_stale_subjective_entries(state, dimensions=dimensions)
```

### 2. **Integration into Review Command** (`desloppify/app/commands/review/entrypoint.py`)

The preflight gate is invoked before the following review modes:
- **Default (prepare)** - line 160
- **--run-batches** - line 101
- **--external-start** - line 112

**NOT invoked for:**
- `--import` - direct import of findings bypasses gate
- `--validate-import` - validation only
- `--external-submit` - submission only
- `--merge` - merge mode

### 3. **Stale Marker Management**

**Two triggering mechanisms:**

#### A. Mechanical Finding Changes (`merge.py`, lines 55-90)
Marks subjective assessments stale when mechanical detectors change:
```python
_DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS = {
    "structural": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    "smells": ("design_coherence", "error_consistency"),
    "coupling": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    # ... etc
}

def _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(state, *, changed_detectors, now):
    payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    payload["refresh_reason"] = "mechanical_findings_changed"
    payload["stale_since"] = now
```

#### B. Review Finding Resolution (`resolution.py`, lines 40-81)
Marks stale when review findings are fixed/wontfixed/marked false_positive:
```python
def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(state, *, status, resolved_findings, now):
    # When review findings change status, mark affected dimension assessments stale
    payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"  # e.g., "review_finding_fixed"
    payload["stale_since"] = now
    payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
```

### 4. **Stale Marker Clearing** (`preflight.py`, lines 19-43)

After the gate passes, `clear_stale_subjective_entries()` removes refresh markers:
```python
def clear_stale_subjective_entries(state, *, dimensions=None):
    """Clear needs_review_refresh markers from subjective assessments."""
    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
        if assessment.get("needs_review_refresh"):
            assessment.pop("needs_review_refresh", None)
            assessment.pop("stale_since", None)
            assessment.pop("refresh_reason", None)
```

### 5. **Command-Line Interface**

**Key flags:**
- `--force-review-rerun` - Bypass the objective-drained check entirely
- `--dimensions <dim1,dim2,...>` - Target specific dimensions (can suggest unscored ones to avoid gate)

**User Messaging:**
When blocked:
```
Blocked: N open objective finding(s) — this is a review rerun.
Scored dimensions: dimension1, dimension2
Tip: target only unscored dimensions with --dimensions logic_clarity
Resolve open items first, or override with --force-review-rerun
```

### 6. **Narrative Reminder** (`reminders.py`, lines 308-331)

The system provides a contextual reminder via `_stale_assessment_reminder()`:
- Only triggers when **all objective findings are resolved** AND stale markers exist
- Suggests: `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions <stale_dims>`
- Tagged as medium-priority reminder with decay (shown max 3 times)

### 7. **Test Coverage** (`test_review_preflight.py`)

Comprehensive test suite covering:
- Blocked when open objectives exist (line 52)
- Allowed when queue empty (line 85)
- `--force-review-rerun` bypasses check (line 93)
- First review (no prior scores) skips gate (line 111)
- Per-dimension targeting logic (lines 170-223)
- Stale marker clearing behavior (lines 276-408)
- Mode-specific gating dispatch (lines 410-557)

### 8. **Related Issue**

Referenced in comments as **issue #157** - "review-rerun preflight gate"

### Key Design Principles

1. **Asymmetric Gate:** Only blocks *reruns* (dimensions with prior scores), not first reviews
2. **Work Queue Integration:** Uses objective-only queue (`include_subjective=False`)
3. **Stale Tracking:** Links assessment staleness to specific mechanical/review finding changes via `refresh_reason`
4. **User Control:** `--force-review-rerun` flag provides escape hatch when needed
5. **Dimension-Aware:** `--dimensions` flag allows partial reruns targeting only unscored dimensions
6. **State Persistence:** Stale markers survive across scans until explicitly cleared by preflight

This creates a clear workflow: resolve all objective findings → gate allows subjective rerun → preflight clears stale markers → fresh review cycle begins.
